Result: 在三个视觉运动操作任务上平均吞吐量达IL基线的2.0倍；在具有交互敏感物体动力学的任务上，达最强加速基线吞吐量的2.2倍。分析显示增益集中在"更快执行会改变机器人-物体演化"的场景。

Conclusion: 示教提供任务意图，play数据提供更快执行该意图所需的动力学覆盖，这一简洁原则被证明有效。

Abstract: Expert demonstrations often specify what a robot should do, but not how fast it can do it. Imitation Learning (IL) inherits demonstration timing, while directly accelerating the learned motion can fail when faster execution changes the robot-object dynamics. We study faster-than-demonstration execution as a dynamics-aware control problem and introduce Expert-Play Contouring Control (EPCC), which combines slow expert demonstrations with fast, non-expert play. Expert demonstrations train a latent trajectory generator whose predictions are reparameterized into a time-independent contour of successful task progression, while play trains a world model (WM) of fast-action outcomes. At deployment, our proposed planner uses the WM to optimize actions that makes maximize progress along the expert-derived contour while penalizing deviation from the intended task evolution. Averaged across three visuomotor manipulation tasks, EPCC achieves a $2.0\times$ the throughput of the IL baseline, including $2.2\times$ that of the throughput of the strongest acceleration baseline on a task with interaction-sensitive object dynamics. Our analysis shows that the gains concentrate where faster execution changes robot-object evolution. Together, our results highlight a simple yet effective principle: demonstrations provide task intent, while play data provides the dynamic coverage needed to execute that intent faster.

</details>


### [37] [ARCGym: Benchmarking Deep Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Robotic Colonoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22803)
*Guanglin Ji,Martina Finocchiaro,Kenny Erleben,Hang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源强化学习环境ARCGym，用于基于图像的柔性结肠镜自主导航基准测试，支持多种机器人类型和临床CT重建结肠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的自主结肠镜导航仿真主要关注全驱动机器人，无法反映临床长柔性结肠镜接触丰富的导航特性。

Method: 构建ARCGym开源强化学习环境与基准，支持胶囊机器人和柔性内窥镜；包含五个CT重建结肠模型、临床相关导航子任务及统一成功指标；提出结合深度管腔对齐与管腔可见性评分的奖励函数。

Result: 跨任务、机器人和解剖结构的实验显示，磁驱动和近端插入柔性机器人的自主导航均仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 近端插入驱动仍是未解决的开放问题，ARCGym为柔性结肠镜自主导航研究提供了基准平台。

Abstract: Simulations for learning-based autonomous colonoscopic navigation focus mainly on fully actuated capsule robots, failing to capture the contact-rich navigation of long and flexible clinical colonoscopes. We present the Autonomous Robotic Colonoscopy Gym (ARCGym), an open-source reinforcement learning environment and benchmark for image-based navigation in clinically derived deformable colon anatomies. ARCGym supports multiple types of colonoscope robots, spanning capsule robots and flexible endoscopes, with this work focusing on flexible endoscopes including magnetic-driven tip actuation and clinically used proximally translational actuation. This work includes five CT-reconstructed colons representing typical clinical scenarios, a set of clinically meaningful navigation subtasks, and unified success metrics. We introduce a reward combining depth-based lumen alignment with a lumen-visibility score to improve learning under occlusions. Experiments across tasks, robots, and anatomies show that autonomous navigation remains challenging for both magnetic-driven and proximal-insertion flexible robots, with proximal-insertion actuation remaining an open problem.

</details>


### [38] [Kinematic Interface for the Wild: Modular Bimanual Loco-Manipulation Capture from 360$^{\circ}$ Cameras Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22809)
*Benjamin Yang,Weiying Wang,Shenggao Li,Keming Yan,Sasha Wilkinson,Zelin Wang,Yip Fun Yeung,Lingfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KIWI是一种仅用现成相机的便携采集套件，将定位与记录任务分给360度相机的后、前两个镜头，实现房间尺度双手操作数据采集，定位成功率99.9%，误差小于4.5毫米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕挂相机需同时完成操作记录与场景定位，但现有手持设备多依赖面向工作区的在线定位（视野易被手和物体遮挡），或需额外跟踪硬件，导致房间尺度双手采集仍需对操作者或场景进行改造。作者希望用纯现成相机、无需改造场景或操作者，实现无共视条件下的双手与头部统一度量定位。

Method: KIWI将两项任务分给360度相机的两个镜头：后镜头朝向房间，构建共享度量地图，将双手及可选头戴相机注册到同一坐标系，无需工作区共视；前镜头记录操作细节，并利用离线IMU融合弥补前镜头跟踪丢失。相机模块通过快拆板可安装在筷子夹爪、平行夹爪、腕部支架或机器人法兰上。

Result: 在六段双手录制中，前后镜头结合仅0.1%的查询帧定位失败；仅用前镜头的双手特征对齐则失败率达24.8%，且有一段录制完全丢失。相对评估基准标记，定位误差保持在4.5毫米以内。恢复出的位姿一致性足够高，仅凭四路腕部流即可将场景重建为3D高斯溅射。

Conclusion: 通过将定位与记录解耦到360度相机的两个镜头上，KIWI证明了仅用现成相机即可实现房间尺度双手采集的高精度定位与场景重建，无需额外跟踪硬件或场景改造。

Abstract: A wrist-mounted camera for UMI-style data collection must do two jobs: record the manipulation and localize in the scene. Most handheld devices localize online from workspace-facing views crowded by hands and objects, or add dedicated tracking hardware. Room-scale bimanual capture therefore still tends to instrument the operator or the scene for accurate localization. We present KIWI (Kinematic Interface for the Wild), a capture kit whose only electronics are off-the-shelf cameras. Our core system splits the two jobs across the two lenses of a 360-degree camera. The rear lens faces the room and builds a shared metric map that registers both hands, and an optional head camera, in one frame without workspace co-visibility; the front lens records the manipulation, and offline IMU fusion bridges front-lens tracking loss. Through our quick-release plate, the camera module attaches to chopstick grippers, parallel-jaw grippers, hand-wrist mounts, or robot flanges. Across six bimanual recordings, combining the rear and front lenses failed to localize only 0.1% of query frames, whereas front-only bimanual feature alignment failed on 24.8% of frames and lost one recording entirely; against evaluation fiducials, localization error stayed within 4.5 mm. KIWI's recovered poses were sufficiently consistent for the four wrist streams alone to reconstruct the scene as a 3D Gaussian splat.

</details>


### [39] [Commonsense-Grounded Path Planning from Abstract Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22813)
*Masafumi Endo,Kohei Honda,Ryo Yonetani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoRS路径规划器，用LLM/VLM推断环境中未明示的常识性考量，对区域排序生成代价并驱动搜索，实现符合常识的路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法只遵守预先写明的考量，但在人群中工作的机器人还必须遵循未明说的考量（如工人在未被提醒时避开湿滑地面），因此需要能推断潜在考量的规划方法。

Method: CoRS利用大语言模型和视觉语言模型作为常识知识：给定抽象指令（如"小心移动"）和各区域视觉观察，为每个区域推导考量描述（如"湿滑地面值得绕行"），再两两比较各区域考量以判断哪个更应避开（如"人群比湿滑地面更糟"），据此将区域排序为常识排序，其代价驱动传统搜索，始终返回有效路线。

Result: 构建了潜在考量下的规划基准，包含3个环境、1350个问题和3个抽象层次的5种指令。实验表明CoRS能发现未明说的考量，绕开值得绕行的区域并穿过其余区域，而近期基于LLM的规划器无法实现这一行为。

Conclusion: CoRS通过LLM/VLM获取常识来推断潜在考量并进行区域排序，驱动搜索生成符合常识的路线，在基准上优于现有LLM规划器。

Abstract: We present \emph{commonsense ranked search} (CoRS), a novel path planner that turns an abstract instruction into a route that follows commonsense. While existing methods respect the considerations written down in advance, a robot working among people must follow those left unstated too, as with a wet floor that a worker avoids without being told. CoRS leverages large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) as commonsense knowledge to reason about these latent considerations in its planning. Given an abstract instruction (\emph{e.g.}, ``move carefully'') and visual observations of each region in the environment, CoRS derives a consideration for each region, as in ``this wet floor is slippery and worth a detour.'' It then compares the considerations between regions to see which of the two the robot should avoid more, as in ``the crowd is worse than the wet floor.'' These judgments sort the regions into a commonsense ranking, whose costs drive a conventional search that always returns a valid route. We build a benchmark for planning under latent considerations, with three environments, 1350 problems, and five instructions at three levels of abstraction. Experiments show that CoRS discovers the unstated considerations and goes around the ones worth a detour while crossing the rest, a behavior that recent LLM-based planners do not achieve.

</details>


### [40] [Whole-Body UMI: Transferring UMI Manipulation Skills to Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation via Real-Time Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22829)
*Yuxuan Nai,Leixin Chang,Liangjing Yang,Shuo Yang,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WB-UMI，一种任务无关、实时的末端执行器条件全身运动生成器，通过共享末端执行器接口将全身协调学习与任务语义学习解耦，实现从原生UMI演示到人形全身操作的可扩展迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采集人形全身操作演示主要依赖遥操作，成本高且难以规模化。UMI虽提供了可扩展的数据采集范式，但仅靠末端执行器轨迹无法充分确定人形全身协调，不足以支持全身演示采集。因此需要一种能解耦全身协调与任务语义学习的方法。

Method: WB-UMI是任务无关、实时、以末端执行器为条件的运动生成器。通过共享末端执行器接口，将全身协调学习与任务语义学习解耦：扩散策略从原生UMI演示中学习，WB-UMI则独立地从重定向运动捕捉中学习，任务数据采集时无需身体追踪器或配对的图像—全身演示。实际部署采用异步层次结构，集成扩散策略、运动生成器和全身控制器，并具备延迟补偿与测量状态反馈。

Result: 在G1真机实验中，实现四类任务的实时闭环迁移：关抽屉成功率90%、货架取放80%、抛球30%、Loco-PnP 40%。

Conclusion: 实验表明该层次结构能有效将原生UMI技能迁移至人形全身操作，验证了通过共享末端执行器接口解耦全身协调与任务语义学习的可行性。

Abstract: Collecting whole-body demonstrations for humanoid manipulation mostly relies on teleoperation, which is costly and hard to scale up. The Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) provides a scalable data collection paradigm, but end-effector trajectories alone underdetermine humanoid whole-body coordination, which is insufficient for whole-body demonstration collection. Therefore, we introduce Whole-Body UMI (WB-UMI), a task-agnostic, real-time and end-effector conditioned motion generator that decouples whole-body coordination learning from task semantics learning through a shared end-effector interface. A diffusion policy learns from native UMI demonstrations, while WB-UMI learns independently from retargeted motion capture, requiring no body trackers or paired image--whole-body demonstrations during task-specific data collection. In real deployment, an asynchronous hierarchy integrates the diffusion policy, motion generator, and a whole-body controller with latency compensation and measured-state feedback. Real-robot experiments on G1 support real-time closed-loop transfer across four tasks, achieving 90% success in drawer closing, 80% in shelf pick-and-place, 30% in ball toss, and 40% in Loco-PnP, which shows the effectiveness of this hierarchy in transferring native UMI skills to humanoid whole-body manipulation.

</details>


### [41] [ForceRFT: Refining VLA Actions through Force-Guided Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22840)
*Yichen Wang,Chaoyang Zhang,Xuqi Su,Jun Ma,Haiyue Zhu,Xiaocong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceRFT：一种力引导残差强化学习框架，在冻结的力条件VLA先验之上学习接触相关修正，结合人类纠正监督与自主任务结果，提升接触密集任务的自主成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力条件VLA策略虽能响应接触，但仅用示范训练时恢复行为受示范覆盖限制，且无法从部署结果中学习；人类纠正模仿提供额外恢复样本，但其目标只匹配局部动作，未显式优化任务回报。

Method: 冻结的示范训练SmolVLA先验生成力条件动作块；轻量残差actor利用执行块期间的腕部反馈修正末端位姿指令。决策时刻腕力旋量、其时间变化与所选基运动共同条件化残差修正与价值估计。人类纠正监督残差actor，验证过的自主转移训练双critic并做价值引导更新，自举限制在自主片段内，避免TD信用跨越人工干预边界。

Result: 在插头插入、环形插销装配、白板擦拭真实机器人实验中，自主成功率高于所评估的示范训练与残差模仿基线；与残差模仿对比支持价值引导残差优化，插头插入消融表明直接使用执行时腕部反馈的收益。

Conclusion: 将人类纠正监督与自主任务结果结合，并对残差策略进行价值引导优化，可有效提升接触密集操作任务的自主恢复能力与成功率。

Abstract: Force-conditioned vision-language-action (VLA) policies can respond to contact, but when trained solely on demonstrations, their recovery behavior may be limited by demonstration coverage, and they do not learn from deployment outcomes. Human corrective imitation provides additional recovery examples, but its objective matches local action targets without explicitly optimizing task return. We present ForceRFT, a force-guided residual reinforcement learning framework that learns contact-dependent corrections from human supervision and autonomous task outcomes. A frozen, demonstration-trained SmolVLA-based prior generates force-conditioned action chunks, while a lightweight residual actor refines individual end-effector pose commands using wrist feedback acquired during chunk execution. The decision-time wrist wrench, its temporal change, and the selected base motion condition both residual correction and value estimation. Human corrections supervise the residual actor, while verified autonomous transitions train the twin critics and support value-guided updates to the same actor. Bootstrapping is restricted to autonomous segments, preventing TD credit from crossing human-intervention boundaries. Real-robot experiments on plug insertion, ring-on-peg assembly, and whiteboard wiping show higher autonomous success rates than the evaluated demonstration-trained and residual-imitation baselines. Comparisons with residual imitation support value-guided residual optimization, while plug-insertion ablations indicate the benefit of direct execution-time wrist feedback.

</details>


### [42] [Physical-Touch Observability from Wrist Wrench in Granular Scooping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22852)
*Hongyi Lin,Song Zhang,Xubo Liu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“物理接触可观测性”概念，验证机械臂腕部六轴力/力矩信号能否在挖掘装载任务中预测最终采集的物料体积，并在6,700次真实机器人挖掘、67种地形的实验中证明正确配对的力/力矩信号显著优于仅动作信息与跨地形错配控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采矿与土方作业是具身智能的重要落地场景，自主运输与驾驶已大幅进步，但装载与铲挖仍依赖熟练人工，带来人员与设备风险。接触前的RGB-D感知仅能反映表面几何，无法获知交互过程中出现的阻力、压实、工具啮合与负载转移等物理状态，因此需要探索交互过程中的力觉信息价值。

Method: 使用6,700次真实机器人铲挖数据，覆盖67种地形，在地形留出（terrain-held-out）测试下，将当前铲斗的六轴腕部力/力矩（腕部力旋量）信号与“仅动作”预测、跨地形错配的力/力矩控制进行对比；并在多种模型架构上使用工程化信号摘要复现结果。

Result: 在回顾性60%序列边界处，正确配对的力/力矩相比仅动作基线将平均绝对误差降低14.2%，相比跨地形错配的力/力矩降低21.9%；工程化信号摘要在不同模型架构下重现该结论。

Conclusion: 腕部力旋量不仅是低层反馈信号，更是一种任务级感知模态，使具身机器人能够在交互中推断隐藏的物理状态，为采矿及其他富接触任务中的“响应感知型”自主作业奠定基础。

Abstract: Mining and earthmoving are important real-world deployment settings for embodied intelligence. Autonomous transport and driving systems have improved substantially, but loading and scooping still often depend on skilled human operators, exposing personnel and equipment to operational risk. For robotic scooping, pre-contact RGB-D sensing reveals surface geometry but not the resistance, compaction, tool engagement, or load transfer that emerge during interaction. We test whether the current scoop's six-axis wrist force/torque (F/T), or wrist wrench, contains information about final collected volume, and whether that information depends on the correctly paired action-terrain interaction. We call this property physical-touch observability. Using 6,700 real-robot scoops across 67 terrains, we evaluate correctly paired current-scoop F/T against pre-contact prediction and correspondence-breaking controls under terrain-held-out testing. At the retrospective 60% sequence boundary, correctly paired F/T reduces mean absolute error by 14.2% relative to Action-only and by 21.9% relative to cross-terrain mismatched F/T. Engineered signal summaries reproduce the result across model architectures. Together, these findings position wrist wrench not merely as a low-level feedback signal, but as a task-level perceptual modality through which embodied robots can infer hidden physical states during interaction, providing a foundation for response-aware autonomy in mining and other contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [43] [SmoLSTM: A Compact Vision-Language-Action Model with Recurrent Memory that Persists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22854)
*Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Parsa Mastouri Kashani,Connor Gäde,Matthias Kerzel,Philipp Allgeuer,Cornelius Weber,Stefan Wermter,Jae Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmoLSTM 用冻结的 SmolVLM 骨干加矩阵记忆 LSTM 控制层，将整个 episode 的观测与动作查询统一为一条不重置的因果流，以 O(1) 循环状态存储实现长程记忆，在 LIBERO-Mem 上取得 85.1% 子目标覆盖率和 77.5% 全任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型通常只依据当前观测预测动作，当任务涉及物体遮挡或视觉上相同的物体时，缺少 episode 历史会导致任务歧义。常见对策是加宽观测窗口，但这会把时间窗口变成超参数，且每步成本随窗口增大而增加。

Method: 提出 SmoLSTM：将冻结的 256M 参数 SmolVLM 骨干与矩阵记忆 LSTM 控制层耦合，观测 token 与动作查询统一在单一因果流中，整个 episode 全程不重置，循环状态存储相对 episode 长度为 O(1)。使用 flow-matching 动作头，在每个控制步预测 10 个末端执行器位姿增量与夹爪命令的动作块。在 140 个任务、7,461 条演示上联合训练单一策略，并在留出初始状态上评估。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Mem 上达到 85.1% 子目标覆盖率和 77.5% 全任务成功率，仅用 0.04B 可训练参数，超过基准自带的对象中心基线及近期基于记忆的方法。若在每个控制步重置循环状态，全任务成功率降至 7.0%，表明策略依赖决策间传递的上下文。同一模型在标准 LIBERO 上平均成功率为 79.6%。

Conclusion: 以循环状态而非加宽观测窗口来承载 episode 历史，可在恒定存储与计算开销下显著提升遮挡/同形物体场景的长程任务表现，且训练得到的策略确实依赖跨决策的循环上下文。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models often predict actions from only the current observation, which can leave tasks involving object occlusion or visually identical objects ambiguous without episode history. The usual countermeasure, widening the observation window, turns the horizon into a hyperparameter and lets per-step cost grow with it. We instead capture the episode in a recurrent state. SmoLSTM couples a frozen 256M-parameter SmolVLM backbone to a matrix-memory LSTM control layer in which observation tokens and action queries are unified in a single causal stream that is never reset throughout the entire episode. Recurrent-state storage is therefore O(1) in episode length. A flow-matching action head predicts chunks of 10 end-effector pose deltas and gripper commands at each control step. Our single policy, trained jointly on 7,461 demonstrations across 140 tasks and evaluated on held-out initial states, performs best, reaching 85.1% subgoal coverage and 77.5% full-task success on LIBERO-Mem with 0.04B trainable parameters, surpassing both the benchmark's own object-centric baseline and recent memory-based approaches. Resetting the recurrent state at every control step reduces full-task success to 7.0%, showing that the trained policy relies on context carried between decisions. The same model achieves 79.6% average success on standard LIBERO.

</details>


### [44] [PileBelief: Persistent Physical State for Interaction-Driven World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22858)
*Hongyi Lin,Song Zhang,Haiquan Liu,Yang Liu,Jinhua Zhao,Xiaobo Qu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PileBelief是一种面向部分可观测挖掘场景的交互驱动持续世界模型，通过保留可见表面之外的长时物理证据，实现多步预测与候选动作排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挖掘场景中每次铲挖都会改变地形并影响后续动作，但局部观测无法完全揭示地下的支撑与物料状态，现有仅依赖当前观测的方法难以准确预测。

Method: 结合观测条件下的物理先验、世界寻址的形变记忆与物理响应记忆；采用动作对齐读取和门控残差修正来改进地形变化与结果预测；部署权重固定时，已完成交互更新实测信念，假设动作推进独立的想象状态。

Result: 相比仅用当前观测的基线，五步联合预测误差降低10.8%，离线动作选择后悔降低65.5%；在Newton/MPM与真实挖掘数据集上，地形变化和铲斗体积预测均有改善。

Conclusion: 持续物理信念是机器人在持续重塑环境中构建世界模型的一种有效表征，可在土壤底层状态未知时实现多步预测与候选动作排序。

Abstract: World models allow robots to anticipate action consequences before execution. This capability is especially valuable in excavation, where each scoop reshapes the terrain and affects subsequent actions. Local observations, however, cannot fully reveal the underlying support and material conditions. We present PileBelief, an interaction-driven persistent world model for partially observed excavation that retains physical evidence beyond the visible surface. It combines an observation-conditioned physical prior with world-addressed deformation memory and physical-response memory. Action-aligned reads and gated residual corrections refine terrain-change and outcome predictions. With deployment weights fixed, completed interactions update measured belief, while hypothetical actions advance a separate imagined state. Compared with a current-observation-only baseline, PileBelief reduces five-step joint prediction error by 10.8% and offline action-selection regret by 65.5%. Experiments on Newton/MPM and real excavation datasets further demonstrate improved terrain-change and bucket-volume prediction. Our method enables multi-step prediction and candidate-action ranking from local observations, even when the underlying soil state is unknown. These results identify persistent physical belief as a useful representation for world models of environments that robots continually reshape.

</details>


### [45] [A Reconfigurable Dual-Opposition Architecture for Single-Hand Assembly and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22871)
*William Su,Yunosuke Nakamura,Yixiao Wang,Yitong Li,Mingrui Yu,Huanan Qi,Boyuan Liang,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可重构双对置架构的灵巧手，实现单手内同时抓取两个零件并完成装配与操作，性能显著优于LEAP Hand。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单手内装配受限于需同时保持对两个独立零件的抓取并控制其相对运动，现有架构难以兼顾装配与手内操作。

Method: 采用四个独立驱动手指组成两个虚拟手指对置，通过可重构手掌控制其相对构型；建立手指与手掌运动学模型及以物体尺寸为条件的工作空间公式；评估手指关节运动与手掌跟踪，并通过抓取、对齐、紧固、按压任务验证装配能力，进行消融实验。

Result: 仿真中对40-230 mm球体连续旋转平均成功率98.6%（LEAP Hand为73.8%）；外部扰动策略微调后多方向扰动下成功率92.8%（LEAP Hand为45.2%）；硬件演示不同尺寸物体的手内旋转。

Conclusion: 该架构可支持单手内两个独立持握零件的装配以及单物体的协调操作，消融实验表明手指外展/内收与手掌重构对成功装配至关重要。

Abstract: In-hand assembly is constrained by the need to maintain grasps on two separate parts while controlling their relative motion within a single hand. To enable both in-hand assembly and manipulation, we present a reconfigurable dual-opposition architecture. Specifically, to support simultaneous grasping of two parts and coordinated in-hand manipulation, four independently actuated fingers are organized into two virtual finger (VF) oppositions, with their relative configuration controlled by a reconfigurable palm. To describe hand motion and simultaneous two-object grasping configurations, a kinematic model of the fingers and palm and an object-size-conditioned workspace formulation are built. To further evaluate motion performance and assembly capability, finger-joint motion and palm tracking are characterized, and in-hand assembly is demonstrated through tasks involving grasping, alignment, fastening, and pressing. Ablation experiments further demonstrate the importance of finger abduction/adduction and palm reconfiguration for successful in-hand assembly. In simulation, the proposed hand achieves a mean continuous sphere rotation success rate of 98.6% over diameters of 40-230 mm, compared with 73.8% for the LEAP Hand. After policy fine-tuning with external disturbances, the proposed hand achieves 92.8% success under disturbances from multiple directions, compared with 45.2% for the LEAP Hand. Hardware demonstrations further show in-hand rotation of objects of different sizes using policies trained in simulation. Together, these results show that the proposed architecture supports both assembly of two separately held parts and coordinated manipulation of a single object within one hand.

</details>


### [46] [Barrier Certificate Synthesis for Non-Polynomial Robotic Dynamics via Polynomial Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22885)
*Shivam Chaubey,Francesco Verdoja,Shankar Deka,Ville Kyrki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用精确多项式提升处理机器人系统中的非多项式（如三角函数）动力学，通过提升域上的联合障碍证书合成与采样数据安全滤波器，在保证安全性的同时提高覆盖率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统需在允许控制输入下保持轨迹处于规定安全集内，障碍证书可提供受控不变区域保证。平方和优化能系统合成此类证书，但直接应用要求多项式动力学，排除了含三角函数等常见机器人非线性，因而需要一种不依赖近似的处理方法。

Method: 采用精确多项式提升，将非多项式动力学替换为受代数约束的多项式增广动力学，保留非线性几何且无近似；构建提升域联合障碍合成以计算带状态反馈控制见证的证书；开发用于零阶保持实现的采样数据安全滤波器；并将样本引导的逐次障碍方法适配到提升表示以评估提升收益的通用性。

Result: 在协调转弯和平面多旋翼模型上，精确提升在两种方法中均提高认证覆盖率：相同样本量下，提升逐次障碍合成以更少障碍和更低计算成本实现更高覆盖率；提升联合障碍合成相比最细测试分段分辨率具有更高覆盖率和更低计算成本。闭环实验中，安全滤波器在所有评估轨迹上保持可行性与安全性，降低空间保守性，并对两个模型减少干预需求。

Conclusion: 精确多项式提升能有效突破障碍证书合成对多项式动力学的限制，在不近似的前提下改善认证覆盖率与计算效率，且安全滤波器在闭环中表现稳定，具有较好的通用性与实用价值。

Abstract: Safe operation of robotic systems requires trajectories to remain within a prescribed safe set under admissible control inputs. Barrier certificates provide such guarantees by certifying a controlled-invariant region within that set. Sum-of-squares optimization offers a systematic way to synthesize such certificates, but its direct application requires polynomial dynamics, excluding common robotic nonlinearities, including trigonometric terms. We address this limitation using exact polynomial lifting, which replaces non-polynomial dynamics with polynomial-augmented dynamics subject to lifting-induced algebraic constraints, preserving nonlinear geometry without approximation. We formulate lifted-domain joint barrier synthesis that computes a certificate with a state-feedback control witness and develop a sampled-data safety filter for zero-order-hold implementation. To assess whether the benefits of lifting persist across synthesis frameworks, we also adapt a sample-guided successive-barrier method to the lifted representation. On coordinated-turn and planar multirotor models, exact lifting improves certified coverage in both methods: at matched sample sizes, lifted successive-barrier synthesis achieves higher coverage with fewer barriers and lower computational cost, while lifted joint barrier synthesis provides higher coverage and lower computational cost than the finest tested piecewise resolution. In closed-loop experiments, the safety filter maintains feasibility and safety across all evaluated trajectories, reduces spatial conservativeness, and requires less intervention for both models.

</details>


### [47] [Stable and Efficient Real-World Online VLA Post-Training via Asynchronous Replay-Anchored Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22888)
*Jiarui Yang,Jiajin Zhang,Bin Zhu,Jingjing Chen,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RAPolicy：一种异步"回放锚定"策略改进框架，用于VLA模型的在线后训练，实现高效机器人交互与稳定策略提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型的在线后训练需要高效利用机器人交互数据，并从持续采集的经验中可靠地改进策略。

Method: 异步并行采样与学习；critic基于回放行为直接从记录动作学习chunk级价值并构建Bellman目标，无需预测下一动作；one-step flow actor复用采样时存储的初始噪声，通过优势加权条件似然学习。

Result: 在真实世界4个单任务与1个五任务联合设置中评估，在线训练仅1–2小时。单任务平均成功率86.3%；联合多任务从52%提升至88%，并保持稳定任务表现、改善较弱能力。

Conclusion: RAPolicy在总体任务成功率上显著优于基线，所需人工干预更少，展现出稳定的策略改进与高在线训练效率。

Abstract: Online post-training of vision-language-action (VLA) models requires efficient use of robot interaction and reliable policy improvement from continually collected experience. We propose asynchronous Replay-Anchored Policy improvement (RAPolicy), a framework that performs rollout and learning concurrently while grounding both critic and actor updates in replayed behavior. The critic learns chunk-level values from recorded actions and constructs Bellman targets without predicting next actions, reducing computation and dependence on action-value estimates outside replay coverage. The one-step flow actor reuses the initial noise stored during rollout and learns through advantage-weighted conditional likelihood, directly supervising the action mapping used for execution. We evaluate RAPolicy across four single-task settings and one joint five-task setting in the real world, with online training budgets of only 1--2 hours. Starting from policies fine-tuned on just 10 demonstrations per task, RAPolicy rapidly adapts to new single tasks and achieves an average 86.3% success rate. In the joint multi-task setting, RAPolicy improves overall success rate from 52% to 88% while preserving performance on already reliable tasks and improving weaker capabilities. Overall, RAPolicy substantially outperforms the baselines in aggregate task success while requiring fewer human interventions, demonstrating stable policy improvement and high online training efficiency. Project page: https://flyfaerss.github.io/RAPolicy.

</details>


### [48] [H-VLA: Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Model with Key-Action Reasoning and Motion Planning in a Unified Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22895)
*Xiongfeng Peng,Lu Xu,Yandong Wang,Jiaqian Yu,Zirui Zheng,Yamin Mao,Weiming Li,Inseop Chung,Hyun-woong Cho,Jaewook Yoo,Dongwook Lee,Daehyun Ji,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层VLA框架H-VLA，将高层关键动作推理与低层运动生成解耦，并采用统一相机中心动作空间与两阶段训练，在仿真与真机任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA方法多采用从语言与视觉观测到密集动作的直接映射，这会削弱预训练VLM继承的语义推理能力，因为VLM主要面向视觉-语言理解而非低层控制；该范式在物体位置、场景布局、机器人本体和相机视角等空间变化下表现脆弱。

Method: H-VLA由三部分组成：关键动作模型（预测下一操作子目标的关键动作）、运动规划模型（以预测的关键动作为条件生成密集未来动作）、统一相机中心动作空间（保证跨数据集、本体与视角的一致表示）；并采用两阶段训练，预训练强调关键动作推理，微调强调密集运动生成。

Result: 在SimplerEnv上，Google Robot视觉匹配达91%、变体聚合84%、WidowX视觉匹配81%；在Agilex真机任务上，相较最强基线在分布内、分布外位置和分布外场景/物体设置下分别提升10、47、16个百分点。

Conclusion: 将高层语义推理与低层动作生成解耦、并配合统一动作空间与分阶段训练，能有效提升VLA模型在空间变化下的鲁棒性与操作性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation, but many existing methods still rely on direct mappings from language and visual observations to dense actions. This formulation can weaken the semantic reasoning capability inherited from pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs), which are mainly optimized for visual-linguistic understanding rather than low-level control, and becomes fragile under spatial variations, including changes in object positions, scene layouts, robot embodiments, and camera viewpoints. To address these limitations, we propose H-VLA, a hierarchical VLA framework that decouples high-level key-action reasoning from low-level motion generation. H-VLA combines a Key-Action Model for predicting a key-action as the next manipulation subgoal, a Motion Planning Model for generating dense future actions conditioned on the predicted key-action, and a Unified Camera-Centric Action Space for consistent representation across datasets, embodiments, and viewpoints. We further adopt a two-stage training strategy that emphasizes key-action reasoning during pre-training and dense motion generation during fine-tuning. Experiments show that H-VLA achieves strong performance on SimplerEnv, reaching 91% on Google Robot visual matching, 84% on Google Robot variant aggregation, and 81% on WidowX visual matching. On Agilex real-robot tasks, H-VLA improves over the strongest baseline by 10, 47, and 16 percentage points under in-distribution, out-of-distribution position, and out-of-distribution scene/object settings, respectively.

</details>


### [49] ["Dear LLaVA, Please Drive": A Depth-Aware Vision-Language Agent for Closed-Loop Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22925)
*Sebastian Berger,Katharina Winter,Fabian B. Flohr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参数高效的VLM自主导航微调方法，采用命令式学习（Imperative Learning）范式，通过优化可微几何代价场而非标注轨迹来学习无碰撞路径，在未见环境中取得有竞争力的SPL且仅更新不到1%参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航框架多依赖模仿学习，需要大量标注轨迹数据，限制了可扩展性与鲁棒性。VLM虽具备丰富的语境理解与泛化能力，但将其用于移动机器人导航仍面临数据需求大、部署成本高的问题。

Method: 提出参数高效的命令式学习范式微调预训练VLM：不依赖标注轨迹，而是针对可微几何代价场进行优化，仅从立体深度观测学习无碰撞路径。构建统一的端到端自然语言驱动机器人控制流水线，共享VLM骨干并搭配任务特定的LoRA模块，实现从语义目标选择到低层轨迹规划的衔接；微调参数不足模型总量的1%。

Result: 在未见环境中取得有竞争力的SPL表现，且更新参数少于1%。真实世界定性实验验证了仿真到现实的泛化能力，无需真实数据微调即可实现稳定路径规划。

Conclusion: 该工作为在移动机器人上部署VLM智能体提供了实用途径，可实现高层语义导航，而无需大规模标注轨迹数据这一高昂前提。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) provide a compelling foundation for reasoning-driven mobile navigation, offering rich contextual understanding and strong generalization from large-scale pretraining. Most existing navigation frameworks rely on imitation learning and therefore require substantial labeled trajectory data, limiting their scalability and robustness. In this work, we propose a parameter-efficient approach to fine-tune a pretrained VLM for autonomous navigation using an Imperative Learning paradigm. By optimizing against differentiable geometric cost fields rather than labeled trajectories, our model learns to generate collision-free paths exclusively from stereoscopic depth observations. We introduce a unified end-to-end navigation pipeline for natural-language-driven robotic control. This system leverages a shared VLM backbone with task-specific Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules, effectively bridging the gap from semantic target selection to low-level trajectory planning. Our approach achieves competitive Success weighted by Path Length (SPL) in unseen environments while updating less than 1% of the model's total parameters. Qualitative real-world experiments validate sim-to-real generalization and stable path planning without fine-tuning on real-world data. These results highlight a practical approach for deploying VLM-based agents on mobile robots, enabling high-level semantic navigation without the prohibitive requirement for large-scale, labeled trajectory data.

</details>


### [50] [Embodied Snap: Octopus-Inspired Distributed Reach-and-Attach with a Speed-Limited Soft Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22926)
*Linxin Hou,Zhihang Qin,Heyang Zou,Qirui Wu,Peiyi Wang,Muhammad Sunny Nazeer,Yongxin Guo,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"具身弹射控制器"，将慢速伺服预载与快速弹性释放分离，使软体臂超越腱驱动速度限制，实现动态"伸达-吸附"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体臂"伸达-吸附"需精准瞄准与足够接触速度，但齿轮驱动执行器存在速度上限，仅靠改进轨迹跟踪无法突破该限制。

Method: 受章鱼生物学启发，采用分节组织先验（非复制章鱼神经系统）。学习策略在三节间共享，选择预载、瞄准、腱松弛与释放时机，决定何处、如何及何时加载与释放身体。策略利用经硬件验证的循环模型、在实验支持的边界内离线优化。

Result: 5个优化种子、400个未见仿真目标下，5 cm横向容差内吸附成功率(73±4)%；共享策略在共同评估预算中位17%时达到匹配的集中式控制器平均最终奖励。硬件表征尖端速度1.56-1.64 m/s，较直接腱驱动释放至少提升108%。18次开环硬件试验（6个位置）中，17次超1 m/s弹射阈值，9次成功取回物体，5个位置成功取回。

Conclusion: 结果展示了控制问题中的实用责任分工：学习控制负责准备身体，被动身体力学执行动态"伸达-吸附"所需的快速运动。

Abstract: Reach-and-attach of soft robotic arms with passive suction requires accurate targeting and sufficient contact speed, yet geared actuators can impose a speed limit that improved trajectory tracking alone cannot overcome. This paper proposes an embodied snap controller that separates slow servo-driven preloading from rapid elastic release, enabling a compliant arm to move beyond its direct tendon-driven speed limit. Octopus biology motivates the controller's section-wise organizational prior, rather than reproduction of the octopus nervous system. A learned policy shared across three sections selects preloads, aim, tendon slack, and release timing, determining where, how, and when to load and release the body. The policy is optimized offline using a hardware-validated recurrent model within experimentally supported bounds. Across five optimization seeds and 400 unseen simulated targets, attachment success is $(73\pm4)\%$ at a $5\text{ cm}$ lateral tolerance, and the shared policy reaches the matched centralized controller's mean final reward after a median $17\%$ of the common evaluation budget. Hardware characterization achieves tip speeds of 1.56-1.64 m/s, at least $108\%$ above direct tendon-driven release. In 18 open-loop hardware trials across six placements, 17 exceed the 1 m/s snap threshold and nine retrieve the object, with successful retrieval at five placements. These results demonstrate a practical division of responsibility in the control problem: learned control prepares the body, and passive body mechanics execute the rapid movement needed for dynamic reach-and-attach.

</details>


### [51] [MIRA-PRM: Mission-Informed Reusable Roadmap Planning for Mobile Gas-Sensing Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22954)
*Gongsen Wang,Siyuan Wang,Xinyuan Wang,Yuhan Wen,Feng Yang,Zhen Tian,Hoi Leong Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向移动气体传感巡检的 MIRA-PRM 概率路线图方法，兼顾几何、任务流与巡检条件采样，在结构化仿真中显著提升有序多点巡检的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动气体传感巡检需要移动平台可靠地按顺序抵达采样位姿，现有 PRM 类方法在有序目标增多、节点预算受限及局部地图变化时成功率明显不足，因此需要一种以可靠性为导向的规划方法。

Method: MIRA-PRM 将几何、任务流与巡检条件三类采样结合，采用局部自适应连接与证据门控式精细化；通过八组开发实验（142,448 次规划运行）评估任务规模、重复查询适应、有限节点预算、目标分布、局部地图修复、消融、跨方法族对比与参数敏感性，并在独立冻结留出集上验证。

Result: 目标由 2 增至 8 时保持 100% 种子级成功率；40 节点上限下达 97.67%，优于 PRM(67.17%)与 PRM*(86.50%)。局部修复使事件耗时降低 79.9-81.9%、新增节点减少 90.8-94.3%。冻结留出集上 96/144 成功，优于 PRM(82/144)、PRM*(89/144)与 LE-HG-PRM(70/144)。配对成功运行中墙钟时间比 PRM* 少 16.89%、比 LE-HG-PRM 少 81.01%，但比 PRM 慢 52.23%；路径长 0.54-1.86%。所有验证路径通过全段检查与间隙非劣性检验。

Conclusion: 结果表明在结构化仿真中 MIRA-PRM 实现了以可靠性为导向的性能权衡，后续需开展外部地图与真实移动气体传感平台的验证。

Abstract: Mobile gas-sensing inspection requires a mobile platform to reliably reach ordered sampling poses. We present MIRA-PRM, a mission-informed probabilistic roadmap that combines geometry-, mission-flow-, and inspection-conditioned sampling with locally adaptive connectivity and evidence-gated refinement. Eight development campaigns comprising 142,448 planner runs evaluated mission scaling, repeated-query adaptation, finite node budgets, target distributions, local map repair, ablation, cross-family comparison, and parameter sensitivity. MIRA-PRM maintained 100% seed-level success as ordered targets increased from two to eight, and achieved 97.67% seed-level success under a 40-node ceiling, compared with 67.17% for PRM and 86.50% for PRM*. Local repair reduced event time by 79.9-81.9% and node additions by 90.8-94.3% relative to rebuilding. On a separate frozen holdout of 12 maps, 48 ordered missions, and three seeds per map-mission unit, MIRA-PRM produced 96/144 validated successes, versus 82/144 for PRM, 89/144 for PRM*, and 70/144 for LE-HG-PRM. On paired common-success runs, MIRA-PRM reduced wall time by 16.89% relative to PRM* and 81.01% relative to LE-HG-PRM, but was 52.23% slower than PRM; paths were 0.54-1.86% longer. All validated paths passed independent full-segment checks and clearance non-inferiority. The results support a reliability-oriented trade-off in structured simulations and motivate external-map and physical mobile-sensing validation.

</details>


### [52] [Prescribed-Time Contracting-Boundary Control of a Tendon-Driven Flexible Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22963)
*Yi Lu,Chao Tang,Zhiji Han,Hongdu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种指定时间性能塑形控制方法，实现三对拮抗肌腱驱动单段柔性臂的曲率跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决直线构型下弯曲方向未定义问题，并实现曲率跟踪的指定时间精度约束控制。

Method: 引入笛卡尔曲率表示与六肌腱运动学映射；构建三次性能边界，结合静态对称误差缩放与时间变行为塑形的双重变换，将跟踪误差映射到固定单位框，设计控制器。

Result: Python与OpenCR-MuJoCo数值评估及两段四通道降阶实验验证；六次试验无边界违反，终端曲率RMSE较基线降低32.5%，终端带进入时间相当。

Conclusion: 结果支持该方法在降阶实验场景下的可行性，可保证边界不变性与指定时间进入终端精度区域并渐近收敛。

Abstract: This study develops a prescribed-time performance-shaping control method for curvature tracking of a single-segment flexible arm actuated by three antagonistic tendon pairs. A Cartesian curvature representation is introduced to avoid the undefined bending direction at the straight configuration and to establish an explicit six-tendon kinematic mapping. A cubic performance boundary contracts smoothly from an initially admissible error bound to a nonzero terminal accuracy bound within a prescribed time. Based on this boundary, a dual transformation combining static symmetric error scaling and time-varying behavior shaping maps the tracking error into a fixed unit box. The resulting controller guarantees boundary invariance, prescribed-time entry into the terminal accuracy region, and subsequent asymptotic convergence. Numerical evaluations with Python and OpenCR--MuJoCo, together with a supervised reduced-order experiment on a two-section, four-channel platform, provide complementary validation. Across six experimental trials, no violation of the prescribed boundary is observed, and the proposed controller reduces the mean terminal curvature RMSE by 32.5% relative to a matched baseline, with comparable terminal-band entry times. These results support the feasibility of the proposed approach in the reduced-order experimental setting.

</details>


### [53] [Transferring the Intelligence of VLMs to Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22966)
*Meng-Hao Guo,Zhe-Han Mo,Jia-Jun Wang,Yi Zhang,Kejin Wang,Yi-Xuan Deng,Jia-Peng Zhang,Yongming Rao,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboDawn，通过人类直观的离散指令接口让视觉语言模型（VLM）在数字与物理世界间迁移智能，实现机器人闭环控制；零样本即超越专用策略，单样本示范后达到 SOTA，并可迁移至真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能无缝适应物理与数字世界，暗示智能本身可跨"数字-现实"鸿沟迁移。由此引出核心问题：VLM 的智能能否同样从数字世界泛化到物理世界用于机器人控制？

Method: 提出 RoboDawn 框架：将机器人控制暴露为一个紧凑的离散动作集（平移、旋转、夹爪命令），供 agentic VLM 使用；VLM 在闭环中观察视觉状态、推理下一步动作、执行并自适应调整。同时引入上下文学习（ICL）方案，用少量示范让 VLM 掌握接口使用与任务解决策略，无需任务特定的机器人训练。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 C2R 上，成功率从零样本 53.2% 提升至单样本 73.6%，超过强基线 π0.5（46.0%）；在 RoboDojo 上从 35.67% 提升至 47.17%。零样本即可超越多个基于基准专用机器人数据训练的强策略，单样本示范后达到 SOTA。框架还迁移到真实 Franka 机器人，完成取块入篮和积木堆叠任务。

Conclusion: VLM 的智能可跨数字-物理鸿沟迁移至机器人控制，无需任务特定训练；简洁直观的接口加少量上下文示范即可实现强性能并泛化到真实场景，验证了跨域智能迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Humans can seamlessly adapt to both physical and digital worlds, suggesting that while a digital-to-real gap exists in embodiment, environment and task, human intelligence itself may transfer across this gap. This naturally raises a fundamental question: can the intelligence of vision-language models (VLMs) similarly generalize from the digital world to the physical world for robotic control? We investigate this question through RoboDawn, a human-intuitive interface that exposes robotic control to an agentic VLM through a compact set of discrete translation, rotation, and gripper commands. Using this interface, the VLM controls a robot in a closed loop: it observes the current visual state, reasons about the next action, executes it, and adapts subsequent decisions to the resulting state. Furthermore, we introduce an in-context learning (ICL) scheme that uses a few demonstrations to ground the VLM in both interface usage and task-solving strategies. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 C2R and RoboDojo demonstrate that RoboDawn achieves strong performance without task-specific robot training. In the zero-shot setting, RoboDawn outperforms several strong policies trained on benchmarkspecific robot data, while a single in-context demonstration further yields substantial performance gains and establishes state-of-the-art (SOTA) results. On RoboTwin 2.0 C2R, the success rate increases from 53.2% zero-shot to 73.6% one-shot, exceeding the solid baseline π0.5 (46.0%). Similar gains are observed on RoboDojo, where success rate improves from 35.67% zero-shot to 47.17% one-shot. The same framework also transfers to real-world robots, performing block-in-basket and block stacking on Franka.

</details>


### [54] [An Empirical Study and Open Testbed for Federated Fine-Tuning of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22973)
*Zhekai Duan,Kevin Ziyang Xie,Xinyu Tan,Shikai Geng,Chengxu Zhou,Ramana Kompella,Gaowen Liu,Chris Xiaoxuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了预训练VLA模型的联邦微调，在LIBERO仿真40任务及两个真实机器人实验上考察联邦范围、聚合算法与分布偏移，并开源测试平台DecentVLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 适配预训练VLA到新机器人、环境或任务需本地采集且常被丢弃的演示数据；联邦学习可利用这些分布式数据学习共享策略，但能否适配大型预训练VLA尚不明确，且缺乏可复现基准与可重用训练框架，导致结果难以比较。

Method: 对三种现代预训练VLA策略进行联邦微调研究：在LIBERO仿真基准的40个任务上，以及两个真实机器人实验的六个真实任务上（演示分别来自两个和三个站点），分析多种联邦参数范围、三种聚合算法，并在分布偏移下评估。

Result: 联邦范围比聚合算法选择更具决定性；真实机器人上难以匹配集中式微调，因跨站异质性比仿真更强；联邦学习在异构数据上可匹配集中式微调，在分布偏移下至少与集中式微调同样鲁棒，并可通过每客户端仅联邦部分策略实现个性化，但此策略在预训练较弱时无可用全局模型。

Conclusion: 联邦VLA学习存在明确机遇与挑战，作者开源模型与运行时无关的测试平台DecentVLA，以促进未来研究与公平比较。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model to a new robot, environment, or task requires demonstrations that are collected locally and often discarded. Federated learning is a promising approach to exploiting such distributed demonstrations by learning a shared policy. However, whether it can adapt large pretrained VLAs remains an open question, and a lack of reproducible benchmarks for pretrained VLAs and reusable training frameworks makes existing results difficult to compare. In this paper, we conduct a systematic study of federated fine-tuning of three modern pretrained VLA policies on the 40 simulated tasks of the LIBERO manipulation benchmark, and on six real-world tasks in two real-robot experiments, with demonstrations collected across two and three sites, respectively. Our study analyzes the key choices in this setting, spanning multiple federated parameter scopes, three aggregation algorithms, and evaluation under distribution shift. Based on the study, we derive a series of lessons, including the dominance of the federated scope over the choice of aggregation algorithm and the difficulty of matching centralized fine-tuning on physical robots, where cross-site heterogeneity is stronger than simulation captures. We also highlight opportunities for federated VLA learning, such as the ability to match centralized fine-tuning on heterogeneous data, to remain at least as robust as centralized fine-tuning under distribution shift, and to personalize, with each client federating part of the policy and keeping the rest local, which helps where the policy's pretraining is weak but leaves no usable global model. We open-source \decentvla{}, the model- and runtime-agnostic testbed behind the study, to facilitate future research and fair comparisons in federated VLA learning.

</details>


### [55] [A Horizon-slicing Approach to Minimum Obstacle Displacement Planning for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22974)
*Antony Thomas,Giulio Ferro,Fulvio Mastrogiovanni,Michela Robba,Marco Baglietto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究最小障碍物移动规划问题，证明其在平面多边形障碍模型下为NP-hard，并提出精确解与近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无碰撞路径初始不存在时，机器人需通过移动可移动障碍物到达目标位置，该问题的计算复杂性尚需明确分析。

Method: 从机器人运动规划视角定义最小障碍物移动问题，给出精确形式化建模，并提出计算强度较低的近似算法。

Result: 证明该问题在平面多边形障碍下为NP-hard；精确解推广了文献中的其他问题；近似解与最优解成本差异为最优成本的一个分数，可在路径长度与障碍物移动量之间权衡。

Conclusion: 最小障碍物移动规划在一般情况下计算困难，但所提近似方法可在计算效率与解质量之间取得平衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the Minimum Obstacle Displacement Planning problem from a robot motion planning perspective. The problem involves determining a feasible path to a goal location by displacing movable obstacles when no collision-free path initially exists. We show that this problem is computationally challenging and, in particular, NP-hard when obstacles are modeled as polygons in the plane. Besides an exact formulation of the minimum obstacle displacement problem generalizing other problems in the literature, and the associated optimal solution, this paper proposes an approximate solution that is less intensive from a computational standpoint, and differs from the optimal solution by a fraction of the optimal cost, being able to trade-off between path length and amount of obstacle displacements.

</details>


### [56] [Connectivity-Aware Exploration of Robotic Grasp Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22983)
*Maksim A Kazanskii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人抓取在SE(3)空间中成功抓取集合的多尺度连通结构，并提出连通性感知采样策略，以更高效地探索可行抓取空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人抓取通常被建模为从候选抓取位姿空间中识别成功动作的问题，但成功动作在该空间中的组织结构（连通结构）较少被关注。作者希望探究可行抓取是否具有可利用的结构，从而提升抓取空间探索效率。

Method: 使用大规模抓取数据集分析可行抓取集合在SE(3)中的连通结构；提出连通性感知采样策略，逐步探索当前观察到的抓取空间，优先考虑组件间的潜在桥接、结构前沿、边界扩展和几何新颖性。

Result: 在受控重建实验中，该方法比随机采样和最远点采样更高效地恢复成功抓取集合的连通结构；进一步验证了隐藏可行性下获得的连通性可改善后续抓取发现，并且先前探索对象的结构经验可被检索并迁移到未见对象。

Conclusion: 可行动作的空间组织提供了超越单个候选动作可行性的抓取空间探索信息，支持将结构感知探索作为利用机器人操作中可行动作空间几何特性的手段。

Abstract: Robotic grasping is typically formulated as the problem of identifying successful actions from a space of candidate grasp poses. However, the organization of successful actions within this space has received less attention. We study the multiscale structure of viable robotic grasps in $SE(3)$ and investigate whether this structure can be exploited for more efficient exploration. Using a large-scale grasp dataset, we show that successful grasp sets exhibit heterogeneous and reproducible connectivity structure across objects. We then introduce a connectivity-aware sampling strategy that incrementally explores the currently observed grasp space by prioritizing potential bridges between components, structural frontiers, boundary extensions, and geometric novelty. In controlled reconstruction experiments, the method recovers the connectivity structure of successful grasp sets substantially more efficiently than random sampling and farthest-point sampling. We further evaluate whether connectivity acquired under hidden grasp viability can improve subsequent grasp discovery, and whether structural experience from previously explored objects can be retrieved and transferred to unseen objects. These results suggest that the spatial organization of viable actions provides information relevant to grasp-space exploration beyond the viability of individual candidate actions. More broadly, they motivate structure-aware exploration as a means of exploiting the geometry of viable action spaces in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [57] [Fast and Robust Temporal Logic Planning via ADMM-based Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23037)
*Lukas Pries,Joris Verhagen,Jon Arrizabalaga,Jana Tumova,Markus Ryll,Zachary Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种快速数值方法，用于在时序逻辑（TL）规范下进行安全的连续时间运动规划，通过将非凸安全与逻辑约束表示为凸集并集并编码于联合可行性图，实现高效的投影与鲁棒性最大化，并在ADMM框架下解耦求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时序逻辑规范下进行连续时间运动规划时，安全约束与逻辑约束具有非凸性，导致求解困难、收敛不可靠且难以扩展到大规模问题，亟需一种既能保证碰撞避免与鲁棒满足任务要求、又能快速求解的数值方法。

Method: 将非凸的安全与逻辑约束表述为凸集的并集，并将相关离散决策编码进一个联合可行性图；借此把到可行集的欧氏投影和近端鲁棒性最大化分别重铸为最短路和最宽路问题。基于该结构，提出一种基于交替方向乘子法（ADMM）的非凸分裂方法，在优化过程中将平滑的时空轨迹优化与非平滑的离散约束处理解耦。方法可生成既无碰撞、又满足时序与逻辑要求的平滑连续轨迹。

Result: 该算法在多个基准上表现出可靠的收敛性，并可扩展到大规模运动规划问题，在离散与连续时间逻辑问题中相比当前最先进方法平均加速4.7倍。

Conclusion: 通过将非凸约束转化为图上的路径问题并结合ADMM分裂，所提方法实现了快速、可靠且可扩展的连续时间时序逻辑运动规划，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: We present a fast numerical method for safe continuous-time motion planning under Temporal Logic (TL) specifications. The method generates smooth continuous trajectories that remain collision-free while robustly satisfying temporal and logical task requirements. A central component of our method is the formulation of nonconvex safety and logic constraints as unions of convex sets where associated discrete decisions are encoded in a joint feasibility graph. This graph representation allows Euclidean projection onto the feasible set and proximal robustness maximization to be reformulated as shortest- and widest-path problems, respectively. Building on this structure, we develop a nonconvex splitting method based on the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM), which decouples smooth spatio-temporal trajectory optimization from nonsmooth discrete constraint handling within the optimization. The resulting algorithm exhibits reliable convergence across benchmarks and scales to large-scale motion-planning problems, providing a 4.7x average speedup over the state of the art on discrete and continuous-time logic problems.

</details>


### [58] [Anatomy of a Closed-Loop Collapse: A Causal Case Study of a Compressed VLA Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23048)
*Fengze Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一篇论文通过因果解剖一个自然出现的压缩策略失败案例，揭示离线评估通过但闭环执行崩溃的现象，并定位其结构化签名及修复方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩操作策略可能通过离线评估却在闭环执行中失败，这一脱节已被先前工作确立，但缺乏对具体失败案例的因果解剖，因此本文旨在剖析一个自然发生的案例以揭示失败的结构与根因。

Method: 对Octo-Base进行8层蒸馏（保留86%参数），在模拟WidowX抓取放置任务上做闭环测试；采用配对动作轨迹取证定位失败签名；在匹配对照下测试四种标准疗法；并进行最小配对干预（将一半训练流替换为部署分布教师rollout）。

Result: 蒸馏模型通过所有离线检查（教师比率0.996和1.000），但闭环中0/72对教师40/72彻底崩溃；失败结构化：早期阶段逐渐退化（移动率90%、抓取率55%），运输阶段0%失败；四种标准疗法均无效；最小配对干预恢复与教师持平（18/36对17/36），消除签名并恢复类教师的扰动响应。

Conclusion: 离线门控（包括家族自身验证指标）不足以作为压缩策略的验收测试；仅需几十次闭环试验即可发现其遗漏的问题。论文主张存在性而非普遍性。

Abstract: Compressed manipulation policies can pass offline evaluation while failing in closed-loop execution; this dissociation is established in prior work and is not our claim. We contribute a causal anatomy of one naturally occurring case. An 8-layer distillation of Octo-Base retains 86% of parameters, passes every offline check we applied (0.996 and 1.000 teacher-ratios on the family's own validation metrics), and collapses in closed loop: 0/72 vs. the teacher's 40/72 on a simulated WidowX pick-and-place task. The collapse is structured, not diffuse: early task stages degrade gradually (the student moves the object at 90% of the teacher's rate and grasps at 55%), while transport-to-target fails categorically, at 0% in every training variant. Paired action-trace forensics isolate the signature: a negative, late-heavy $z$ residual, roughly 10x its post-repair magnitude, and persistent across the base distillation and both continuation branches. Four standard therapies fail under matched controls: continued training and in-domain offline data leave success at zero, even though the latter measurably improves marginal action statistics; command-level compensation recovers nothing at any offset, although the same perturbations degrade healthy policies; clamping the symptom in the command channel preserves grasping, yet success stays at floor. A minimal-pair intervention that substitutes half of the training stream with deployment-distribution teacher rollouts, with every other setting held fixed, restores parity with the teacher (18/36 vs. 17/36 held-out), eliminates that signature, and recovers a teacher-like perturbation-response profile. We claim existence, not universality. Operationally, offline gates, including a family's own validation metrics, are insufficient acceptance tests for compressed policies; a few dozen closed-loop trials sufficed to find what they missed.

</details>


### [59] [On the Control of Mobile Ad-Hoc Agent Deployments in Partially Observed Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23060)
*Edwin Meriaux,Louis-Roy Langevin,Shuo Wen,Ndiamé Ndiaye,Gregory Dudek,Antonio Loría*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究未知正交环境下的移动自组网在线部署问题，形式化为POCGAG问题，证明了CADENCE算法的最优部署界，并评估了优化启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动自组网在未知正交环境中的在线部署缺乏理论保证，需要研究覆盖与连通性的算法边界。

Method: 将问题形式化为部分可观测协作守卫画廊问题（Partially Observable Cooperative Guard Art Gallery Problem），对CADENCE算法进行完整证明，并评估降低智能体数量和部署时间的部署顺序启发式。

Result: 证明CADENCE算法在含n个角点、h个孔的正交世界中，使用至多n/2 + h - 2个智能体即可实现全覆盖并保持连通的可见性图；启发式方法在实践中减少智能体数量与部署时间。

Conclusion: CADENCE算法为未知正交环境下的自组网部署提供了理论保证，启发式策略可在实践中进一步提升效率。

Abstract: We study the online deployment of mobile ad hoc networks in unknown orthogonal environments, formalized as the Partially Observable Cooperative Guard Art Gallery Problem. We give a full proof that CADENCE algorithms achieve full coverage while maintaining a connected visibility graph using at most $n/2 + h - 2$ agents in orthogonal worlds with $n$ corners and $h$ holes. We further evaluate deployment-order heuristics that reduce agent count and deployment time in practice.

</details>


### [60] [Splat-CBF: Safe Next-Best-View Control in 3D Gaussian-Splat Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23100)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出Splat-CBF，一种主动感知控制障碍函数，将相机朝向下一最佳视角与硬约束碰撞避免统一于二次规划框架，在室内仿真、Isaac Kinova机械臂与Ackermann驱动机器人上验证其更快、信息更多、在线运行更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在未建图环境中导航需同时探索与避障，两者存在冲突：最值得观测的区域恰是地图最不确定、碰撞裕度最不可信之处。如何在这一张力下兼顾安全与感知是核心问题。

Method: 提出Splat-CBF主动感知控制障碍函数。安全由风险感知CBF实现，将高斯场的平均风险价值（AVaR）转化为单一平滑硬约束；感知由第二个障碍函数实现，奖励机器人规划路径附近高期望Fisher信息增益的相机朝向。二者在二次规划中结合：安全为硬约束，感知为软约束，其松弛惩罚根据感知已被放松的频率与机器人距不确定区域的远近自适应调整。

Result: 在室内仿真、Isaac Kinova机械臂及Ackermann驱动机器人实验中验证：相较仅安全与仅感知基线，机器人导航更快、获取信息更多、在线运行更快，且仅在安全需要时才放弃有信息量的运动。

Conclusion: Splat-CBF通过硬安全约束与自适应软感知约束的QP融合，有效化解了探索与避障的冲突，实现了安全与感知兼顾的高效导航。

Abstract: Where to look and how to move? A robot navigating an unmapped environment must do both at once, and the two goals pull against each other. The regions most worth observing are the ones the map knows least about, and those are exactly where the robot cannot trust its collision margins. We resolve this tension by introducing Splat-CBF, an active perception control barrier function that steers the camera toward the next best view while collision avoidance is enforced as a hard constraint. Safety is enforced by a risk-aware control barrier function that turns the Average Value-at-Risk of the Gaussian field into a single smooth hard constraint. Perception is enforced by a second barrier that rewards camera orientations with high expected Fisher information gain near the robot's planned path. The two meet in a quadratic program where safety is hard and perception is soft, with a slack penalty that adapts to how often perception has already been relaxed and how close the robot is to an uncertain region. We verify the method in indoor simulations, a Isaac Kinova manipulator and in experiments on an Ackermann-drive robot. Our results assert that robot navigates faster, gathers more information, and runs faster online than safety-only and perception-only baselines, giving up informative motion only when safety requires it.

</details>


### [61] [DiagGen: Agentic Generation of Deformable Assets with Sim-based Diagnostics for Robotic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23103)
*Guanxiong Chen,Yiduo Qu,Qianjun Xia,Pengyu Jing,Yixian Cheng,Bole Ma,Pengzhi Yang,Bingyang Zhou,Ziming Li,Shashwat Suri,Gongbo Sun,Chao Liu,Peter Yichen Chen,Ziqiu Zeng,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiagGen是一个智能体框架，通过"生成—模拟—诊断—细化"闭环，将单张野外图像转化为可仿真的可变形资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成框架通常在生成后才评估物理合理性，未将物体的模拟响应用作修复上游错误的反馈。

Method: 构建部件感知的几何与材质参数；用基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的智能体选择语义上有信息的区域，在物理仿真器中探测、观察材质响应，并将有证据支持的修复线索路由到相应的生成阶段。

Result: 在40个资产上的实验表明，诊断能提供有用的修复线索，并能适度提升生成的可变形资产质量；生成的资产可直接用于高保真物理仿真器中的接触密集型抓取放置任务的规划与仿真，而视觉基础模型生成的资产可能无法仿真。

Conclusion: 将仿真响应作为诊断反馈可改善可变形资产生成，并使资产可直接用于下游机器人操作仿真。

Abstract: While simulation-ready deformable assets are essential for in-silico robotic manipulation tasks, existing generation frameworks typically assess physical plausibility after generation, leaving an object's simulated response unused as feedback for repairing upstream errors. We present DiagGen, an agentic framework that turns a single in-the-wild image into a simulation-ready deformable asset through a generate--simulate--diagnose--refine loop. DiagGen constructs part-aware geometry and material parameters, then uses a VLM (vision-language model)-based agent to select semantically informative regions, probe them in a physics simulator, observe material responses, and route evidence-backed repair cues to the responsible generation stage. Experiments on 40 assets show that diagnostics provides useful repair cues and can moderately improve the quality of generated deformable assets. Finally, we show that unlike assets generated from visual foundation models which may not be simulatable, DiagGen-generated deformables can be directly dropped into a high-fidelity physical simulator for the planning and simulation of contact-rich pick-and-place tasks. The project's website is https://diaggen.github.io/.

</details>


### [62] [Search, Ground, Plan: Functional Sufficiency for Task and Motion Planning under Incomplete Scene Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23113)
*Narendhiran Vijayakumar,Nav Singhal,Girish Varma,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRAB-TAMP 是一个基于基础模型的 TAMP 框架，先搜索并确认场景中满足功能角色的物理对象，再在功能充分性得到完整联合分配后规划，从而提升任务与场景之间的匹配度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于基础模型的任务与运动规划虽能处理语言与视觉观测下的操作问题，但由于场景知识不完整，任务需求与物理场景能否真正实现之间存在关键缺口。

Method: 用功能角色、关系与分配约束表示任务；在需求未解决时增量式检查场景，通过语义、几何和关系校验验证候选对象；仅在完整联合分配建立功能充分性后进行规划。

Result: 在 Kitchen、Living Room、Workshop 三类域的 32 种场景变体、200 次可行试验中，端到端成功率为 54.0%，规划目标覆盖率为 67.3%；相比三个同类 FM 基 TAMP 框架，端到端成功率较基线均值提高 25.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: GRAB-TAMP 通过显式搜索并验证满足任务功能需求的场景实体，有效缩小任务理解与物理可实现性之间的差距，在多个域中显著优于现有 FM 基 TAMP 方法。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have expanded task and motion planning (TAMP) to manipulation problems specified through language and visual observations. However, incomplete scene knowledge leaves a critical gap between understanding what the task requires and knowing whether the physical scene can actually realize it. We introduce GRAB-TAMP, an FM-based TAMP framework that searches for scene entities required for task completion, grounds functional roles to valid physical objects, and plans only after a complete joint assignment establishes functional sufficiency. We represent the task through functional roles, relations, and assignment constraints, and incrementally inspect the scene while requirements remain unresolved, verifying candidate objects through semantic, geometric, and relational checks. We evaluate GRAB-TAMP across 32 scene variants spanning Kitchen, Living Room, and Workshop domains. Across 200 feasible trials, our approach achieves 54.0% end-to-end success with 67.3% plan goal coverage. Compared with three FM-based TAMP frameworks under the same execution setting, GRAB-TAMP improves end-to-end success by 25.7 percentage points over the mean baseline. Implementation and evaluation code: https://github.com/Narendhiranv04/GRAB-TAMP

</details>


### [63] [Verti-WM: A Physics-Aided Exteroceptive World Model for Off-Road Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23118)
*Chenhui Pan,Tong Xu,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Verti-WM，一种物理辅助的外部感知世界模型，将冻结Transformer（刚性地形）与神经符号地形力学模型（可变形地形）递归融合，用于越野导航策略优化，大幅提升预测精度与训练效率并成功迁移到真实平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野导航的强化学习依赖大量车辆-地形交互数据，高保真仿真中采集成本极高。世界模型可替代仿真器 rollout，但越野场景要求状态转移以外部感知的地形信息为条件（仅本体感受不足），且需同时建模刚性与可变形地形，数据驱动与物理方法各有优劣，单一方法难以胜任。

Method: Verti-WM 是一个物理辅助的外部感知世界模型：以递归方式融合两个模块——用于刚性地形的冻结 Transformer，以及用于可变形地形的神经符号地形力学（terramechanics）模型；在每个预测位姿处从给定地图查询高程与语义观测作为融合条件，从而支持六自由度 rollout 用于策略优化，无需再访问仿真器。

Result: 相比数据驱动与物理基线，预测误差分别降低 34.6% 和 21.7%；完全在 Verti-WM 内训练的策略任务成功率相当，而计算时间相比在高保真仿真器直接训练减少 23.6 倍。真实数据验证显示可在学到的真实运动动力学中进行策略优化，在 Verti-4-Wheeler 平台上达到 80% 成功率，而直接 sim-to-real 迁移仅 40%。

Conclusion: 物理辅助的外部感知世界模型能有效结合数据驱动与物理建模优势，在无需高保真仿真器的情况下实现高效且可迁移到真实世界的越野导航策略优化。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for off-road navigation requires extensive vehicle-terrain interaction data, which are costly to collect in high-fidelity simulation. World models offer a promising alternative by replacing simulator roll-outs during policy optimization. However, an off-road world model must condition state transitions on exteroceptive terrain information, which proprioception alone does not provide. This challenge is further amplified by the need to model both rigid and deformable terrain, where data-driven and physics-based approaches offer complementary strengths. We propose Verti-WM, a physics-aided exteroceptive world model that recurrently fuses a frozen Transformer for rigid terrain and a neuro-symbolic terramechanics model for deformable terrain. Elevation and semantic observations queried from a supplied map at each predicted pose condition fusion, enabling six-degree-of-freedom rollouts for policy optimization without further simulator access. Verti-WM reduces prediction error by 34.6% and 21.7% over data-driven and physics-based baselines, respectively. Policies trained entirely within Verti-WM achieve comparable task success rates while reducing computation time by 23.6X relative to direct training in the high-fidelity simulator. We further validate Verti-WM using real-world data, enabling policy optimization within learned real-world kinodynamics and achieving a 80% success rate on the Verti-4-Wheeler platform, compared with 40% for direct sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [64] [Selective Commitment for Language-Guided Object Retrieval under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23131)
*Wonhee Koh,Sushil Samuel Dinesh,Hansol Ko,Shinkyu Park,Eungjoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个闭环框架，用于部分可观测下的语言引导目标检索，协调证据收集、交互、抓取与弃权决策，在仿真中优于基线，并在真机上验证了闭环重观测与失败恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测条件下，语言引导的目标检索不仅需要识别目标，还需决定是否继续收集证据、与场景交互、抓取候选或弃权。现有方法难以在统一框架中协调这些决策，尤其当目标与参考容器存在关联时更具挑战。

Method: 维护对目标身份、容器关系与存在性的联合信念，包含被跟踪目标、未观测目标与目标缺席三类假设；利用视角条件化的类别式VLM观测更新信念；以保形抓取资格（conformal grasp eligibility）与机器人可行性约束来决定是否承诺抓取；并通过有限时域信念空间规划选择信息收集动作。

Result: 在五类场景中，方法在19/25仿真回合成功，最佳任务自适应基线为12/25，且是唯一在每个场景中至少成功一次的策略。消融显示跨视角记忆在部分遮挡下提升成功率，但完整系统并不总是优于简化变体。真机实验显示可闭环重观测并从注入的抓取失败中自主恢复，而注入的视角失败则导致错误弃权。

Conclusion: 结果证明了在部分可观测下，将证据收集与选择性抓取承诺统一协调于单一框架中的可行性。

Abstract: Language-guided object retrieval under partial observability requires deciding whether to gather more evidence, interact with the scene, grasp a candidate, or abstain. We present a closed-loop framework that coordinates these decisions for retrieving a target specified in relation to a reference container. The framework maintains a persistent joint belief over target identity, container relation, and presence through tracked-object, unobserved-target, and target-absent hypotheses. View-conditioned categorical VLM observations update this belief; conformal grasp eligibility and robot feasibility govern commitment, while finite-horizon belief-space planning selects information-gathering actions. Across five different scenarios, our proposed method succeeds in 19/25 simulation episodes versus 12/25 for the best-performing task-adapted baseline and is the only evaluated policy to achieve at least one success in each scenario. Ablations show that cross-view memory improves success under partial occlusion, while the full system does not consistently outperform simplified variants. Real-robot trials demonstrate closed-loop re-observation and autonomous recovery from injected grasp failures, while injected viewpoint failures end in false defer. Experimental results demonstrate the feasibility of coordinating evidence gathering and selective grasp commitment within a unified framework for retrieval under partial observability.

</details>


### [65] [Tip Manipulation in Soft Everting Robots via Wall Retraction and Deployable Fingers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23132)
*Nelson Badillo Perez,Niccolo Pagliarani,Matteo Cianchetti,Robert D. Howe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于壁面回缩的软外翻机器人尖端操控与多工具部署策略，实现膜上器械的按需定位与可逆尖端操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软外翻机器人虽能沿狭长受限路径持续生长，但主动交互仅局限于固定在尖端或其附近的单一工具，无法按需重新定位，且难以与柔软本体协调。

Method: 采用壁面回缩策略：基座滚轮组件独立计量外壁膜流动，尾部卷轴调节内部尾段生长；通过壁面与尾部协同驱动解耦机器人长度与膜材料位置，使膜载器件可被输送、暴露并在近尖端处重定位。同时集成超轻褶皱充气手指（TPU涂层尼龙，内置气密气囊）。

Result: 褶皱设计手指在50 kPa下弯曲约100度，阻塞力可达1.9 N，运输时保持柔软；系统实现对多种家用物体（21–550 g，16–200 mm）的自适应抓取、三维操作与堆叠、环境支撑伸展、受限空间检查的远端相机平移，以及受控的序列化载荷递送。

Conclusion: 该结果为软生长机器人在杂乱、曲折环境中的具身与可逆尖端操控提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Soft everting robots can traverse long, confined paths by continuously growing, yet active interaction remains limited to a single tool fixed at or near the tip, unable to be repositioned on demand and difficult to reconcile with the robot's soft body. We introduce a tip-manipulation and multi-tool deployment strategy for soft everting robots based on wall retraction, implemented with a base roller assembly that independently meters membrane flow in the outer wall while a tail spool regulates growth in the internal tail section. Coordinated wall and tail actuation decouples robot length from membrane-material position, enabling membrane-mounted devices to be transported, exposed, and repositioned at selected locations near the distal tip. We pair this capability with ultralight pleated inflatable fingers integrated into the membrane, fabricated from TPU-coated nylon with an internal airtight bladder. The fingers achieve large bending at low pressures (approximately 100 degrees at 50 kPa in high-pleat designs) and generate blocking forces up to 1.9 N, while remaining limp during transport. The system demonstrates adaptive grasping across diverse household objects (21 to 550 g; 16 to 200 mm), three-dimensional object manipulation and stacking, environmentally braced extension, distal camera panning for confined-space inspection, and controlled sequential payload delivery. These results enable embodied and reversible tip manipulation for soft growing robots in cluttered and tortuous environments.

</details>


### [66] [AquaCap: A Training-Free Underwater Embodied Agent with Code-as-Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23133)
*Xiaoshi Li,Yule Xu,Chunghiu Kong,Yizhou Zhou,Yang Liu,Hao Yang,Zihao Huang,Yunxiao Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练代码即策略框架AquaCap，实现水下自主导航与操作，仿真成功率66.43%并完成真实ROV抓取与运输实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型依赖大规模交互数据，而水下数据采集成本高、样本稀缺，限制了其在水下具身智能中的应用。

Method: AquaCap采用双层智能体，将任务指令与环境观测转化为条件感知规划与可执行控制程序；通过结构化感知在退化水下条件下提供语义、几何与可靠性感知观测；并利用失败感知记忆诊断失败动作，支持闭环重规划与代码修订，实现在线自适应且无需任务特定训练或参数更新。

Result: 在仿真中达到66.43%的成功率；真实世界实验中，ROV完成了自主抓取与物体运输，包括对被水动力扰动移位目标的操控。

Conclusion: 免训练的代码即策略框架可有效应对水下数据稀缺问题，具备仿真与真实环境中的自主导航与操作能力，为水下具身智能提供可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language-action models have stimulated growing interest in underwater embodied intelligence. However, their reliance on large-scale interaction data limits their applicability underwater, where data collection is costly and scarce. To address this challenge, we present AquaCap, a training-free Code-as-Policy framework for autonomous underwater navigation and manipulation. AquaCap employs a dual-layer agent that translates task instructions and environmental observations into condition-aware plans and executable control programs. Structured perception then provides the agent with semantic, geometric, and reliability-aware observations under degraded underwater conditions. A failure-aware memory diagnoses unsuccessful actions and supports closed-loop replanning and code revision. This design enables online adaptation without task-specific training or parameter updates. AquaCap achieves a 66.43% success rate in simulation. Real-world experiments further demonstrate autonomous grasping and object transport with an ROV, including the manipulation of targets displaced by hydrodynamic disturbances.

</details>


### [67] [Probabilistic Scene Graphs: Hierarchical Representation and Real-time System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23144)
*Waqas Ali,Michele Antonazzi,Timon Homberger,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Patric Jensfelt,Yixi Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出概率场景图（PSG）及其实例化方法HGG，将不确定性作为显式信念在图的构建与优化中传播，实现传感器速率运行与近恒定内存，并在多数据集上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图虽提供语义丰富、层次化的机器人感知表示，但未能将不确定性作为显式信念维护，也未在构图和精化过程中传播不确定性。

Method: 提出PSG，将后验分解为离散图结构（实体、关系、语义属性）与连续空间状态，不确定性在两者上均保持。几何直接由节点承载。以高斯层次图（HGG）实例化空间接地：物体基元用全协方差高斯在Normal-Inverse-Wishart信念下表示，并递归参数化生成几何图以细化表面。构建建图流水线：基于图的由粗到细对齐进行配准，嵌套EM与因子图优化联合精化位姿、物体参数与内部几何。

Result: 在涵盖室内RGB-D、室外LiDAR及跨模态部署的六个数据集上，HGG以传感器速率运行、内存近恒定，实现领先的物体精度与零样本图对齐。

Conclusion: PSG将不确定性显式融入场景图的构建与精化全过程，几何由节点承载并使度量地图从图中派生，HGG验证了该框架的高效性与优越精度。

Abstract: 3D scene graphs provide semantically rich and hierarchical representations for robot perception. However, existing systems do not maintain uncertainty as an explicit belief or propagate it through the operations that construct and refine the graph. We introduce Probabilistic Scene Graph (PSG), a generalization of the conventional scene graph that represents a posterior over possible graphs, factorized into a discrete graph structure of entities, relations, and semantic attributes, and continuous states that ground them spatially, with uncertainty maintained over both components. Geometry is carried directly by the nodes rather than selected from a separately constructed metric map, so a metric map, where needed, follows from the graph rather than preceding it. We instantiate PSG's probabilistic spatial grounding with hierarchical graphs of Gaussians (HGG): each object primitive is represented by a full-covariance Gaussian under a Normal-Inverse-Wishart belief, and the same parametrization applied recursively within a node yields a geometry graph that resolves its surface at finer resolution. We then build a mapping pipeline that preserves these beliefs throughout graph construction and refinement: a purely graph-based coarse-to-fine alignment registers observations by comparing node beliefs, while a nested Expectation-Maximization and factor-graph optimization jointly refines poses, object parameters, and internal geometry. Across six datasets spanning indoor RGB-D, outdoor LiDAR, and cross-modality deployment, HGG operates at sensor rate with near-constant memory and achieves state-of-the-art object accuracy and zero-shot graph alignment.

</details>


### [68] [Robot World Models Are Not Invariant to How the Actions Are Written](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23252)
*Ahmed Karim,Leon Chlon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 世界模型会隐式继承动作参数化选择（绝对关节目标 vs 相对增量），这种继承是灾难性的：同一 commanded 轨迹换种编码就会导致检索退化 2.6-13.4 倍、目标选择从 53% 掉到 15%，但两种编码在给定 joint 输入下互相可重构（R²=0.996），并非信息损失。修复方式是在目标层对两种编码取平均，而非输出层平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略训练时需在两类动作参数化（绝对关节目标或相对当前状态的增量）中做选择，这是工程决策；而条件于动作的世界模型会静默继承该选择。作者要揭示这种继承带来的严重后果，并区分它究竟是普通的分布偏移，还是更本质的缺陷。

Method: 在三个机器人数据集、两种形态上训练潜动力学模型，将同一 commanded 轨迹用另一种参数化喂入做交叉测试；用 R²（互相可重构性）与正交性（cos 相似度）判别编码是否损失信息；提出区分"合法重参数化 / 有损摘要 / 传感器替换"的检验，并比较目标层平均与输出层平均两种修复路径。

Result: 检索退化 2.6-13.4 倍；目标条件动作选择 53%→15%；PushT 上同一未来的两种信念近乎正交（cos=0.067，最差 -0.377）。在 R²=0.996 下可互相重构，说明无信息损失；所提检验在 4 条候选轴中否决了 3 条。目标层平均可自行恢复任务性能（输出层平均对方向型预测不可用，归一化均值可能低于轨道中每个成员）；目标平均遗留最差一致性 0.78，加不一致惩罚升至 0.995。但 PushT 上单靠平均无法修复该轴。

Conclusion: 缺陷位于动作通道，是视觉模型不变性研究未审查的盲区（该领域只关注裁剪、抖动、相机位姿）。参数化审计应被纳入世界模型评估；修复优先在目标层取平均，并辅以不一致惩罚以保住尾部最差情形，但某些轴（如 PushT）需额外手段。

Abstract: A robot policy is trained with one of two action parameterizations: absolute joint targets, or deltas relative to the current state. The choice is a live engineering decision in robot learning, and a world model conditioned on actions inherits it silently. We show the inheritance is catastrophic. A latent dynamics model trained on one parameterization and handed the identical commanded trajectory written in the other collapses: retrieval degrades by 2.6-13.4x across three robot datasets and two morphologies, goal-conditioned action selection falls from 53% to 15%, and on PushT the two beliefs about the same future are near-orthogonal (cos = 0.067, worst case -0.377), so the predictor does not degrade gracefully, it answers a different question. This is not a distribution-shift artifact in the usual sense: the two encodings are mutually reconstructible at R^2 = 0.996 given the joint input, so no information is lost, and we give the test that separates a valid re-parameterization from a lossy summary or a sensor swap. The test rejected three of the four axes we proposed. The defect lives in the action channel, which the invariance literature for visual models does not examine: work there concerns crops, jitter and camera pose, while the parameterization of the commands goes unaudited. The repair is averaging over the two encodings, and where it goes matters. Averaging the objective restores task performance by itself; averaging the outputs, safe for probabilities by concavity, is not available for direction-valued prediction, where the normalized mean can score below every member of the orbit. What objective-averaging leaves behind is the tail: worst-case agreement stays at 0.78, a disagreement penalty closes it to 0.995, and over a latent rollout it is the difference between a worst case that erodes and one that holds. On PushT, averaging alone does not repair the axis.

</details>


### [69] [Scenario MPC with STL Specifications and Pareto-Based Feasibility Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23263)
*Tianhao Wu,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将可行性修复视为Pareto优化的MPC框架，用于处理随机且不可控多智能体下的STL约束，并提供STL违反率的概率证书，在自动驾驶场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实多智能体应用（如自动驾驶）常涉及随机且不可控的智能体，而现有STL控制合成多假设智能体协作且动力学已知。已有鲁棒控制工作存在不足：要么只保证严格满足STL约束而不处理不可行恢复，要么以自我中心目标放松不可行约束，缺乏对智能体间目标权衡的显式刻画与不确定性量化。

Method: 提出一种模型预测控制（MPC）框架，将可行性修复视为Pareto优化问题，以显式刻画多智能体目标之间的权衡；同时提供关于STL违反率的概率证书，形式化量化在随机且不可控智能体下的不确定性。

Result: 在两个自动驾驶场景中评估该框架，结果表明该框架能够恢复可行控制，并展现出安全行为。

Conclusion: 将可行性修复建模为Pareto优化并结合STL违反率概率证书，可在随机不可控智能体环境中实现兼顾权衡与不确定性量化的安全控制，且适用于自动驾驶类场景。

Abstract: Temporal logic is a formal language for reasoning about system behaviors over time. Signal temporal logic (STL), in particular, has been used to encode spatio-temporal requirements for control synthesis in multi-agent systems, often under the assumption that agents are cooperative and their dynamics are known. However, real-world multi-agent applications, such as autonomous driving, typically involve stochastic and uncontrollable agents. Recent work explored robust control with worst-case or probabilistic formulations, but remains limited in that it either (1) certifies strict satisfaction of STL constraints without addressing feasibility recovery, or (2) relaxes infeasible constraints with ego-centric objectives. In this paper, we propose a model predictive control (MPC) framework that treats feasibility repair as a Pareto optimization problem to explicitly characterize tradeoffs among agent objectives. We further provide a probabilistic certificate on STL violation rate to formally quantify uncertainty under stochastic and uncontrollable agents. The proposed framework is evaluated on two autonomous driving scenarios. Results show that the framework recovers feasible control with demonstrated safe behaviors.

</details>


### [70] [Latent Telepathy: Multi-Robot Communication with Self-Supervised Perceptual Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23269)
*Howard Wang,Han Zheng,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Telepathy：机器人直接广播自身感知用的冻结自监督编码器潜向量作为通信内容，零额外计算、低带宽且语义一致，可让智能体在遮挡下高效协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测的分散式多机器人团队中，决定动作的关键信息往往只有队友能看到。现有分散方法只传位置或轨迹等运动学信息，无法传达感知内容；MARL 学习通信虽能携带感知，但消息与任务耦合且不可解释。

Method: 每台机器人广播其自身感知已计算出的潜向量——该向量来自以自监督联合嵌入预测目标训练、冻结且团队共享的编码器。接收方仅凭任务奖励学习如何使用它。采用内容受控协议，固定带宽、延迟、拓扑与接收方，只变化消息内容进行对比。

Result: 广播潜向量使导航者避开被遮挡危险的成功率达 99.7%，与无噪声手工设计消息相当；位置和轨迹消息仅随机水平；宽 186 倍的原始相机图像反而更不可靠。结果从离散网格世界扩展到连续速度控制的渲染像素场景，且编码器在真实机器人相机上于 102/102 次实时决策中解出危险。另指出把 MARL 通信结果迁移到连续控制的需求：消息所支撑的决策必须仍可通过探索到达，并给出恢复方法。

Conclusion: Latent Telepathy 用现成感知潜向量作为通信，实现零额外计算、单向量带宽、跨机器人语义一致且对接收方不透明的高效协作，并揭示连续控制迁移的关键条件。

Abstract: In a decentralized multi-robot team under partial observability, the fact that decides a robot's next action is often visible only to a teammate. Existing decentralized methods communicate kinematic information, such as position or planned trajectory, which cannot convey what the teammate perceives. Learned communication in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) can carry perceptual content, but the resulting messages are task-coupled and opaque. We propose Latent Telepathy. Each robot broadcasts the perceptual latent vector it already computes for its own use, the output of an encoder trained with a self-supervised joint-embedding predictive objective, frozen, and shared across the team. A teammate learns to act on it from task reward alone. Because the encoder already runs for perception, the message costs no additional computation and a single compact vector of bandwidth. Because the encoder is frozen before any policy is trained, the message means the same thing to every robot, and the receiving robot is never told what it means. We evaluate Latent Telepathy with a content-controlled protocol in which bandwidth, latency, topology and receiver are held fixed and only the message content varies. Broadcasting the latent lets a navigator avoid an occluded hazard in 99.7% of episodes, matching a noiseless hand-designed message. Position and trajectory messages remain at chance, and the raw camera image, 186 times wider, is less reliable than the compressed latent. The result holds from a discrete gridworld to rendered pixels under continuous velocity control, and the encoder decodes the hazard from a physical robot's camera in 102 of 102 live decisions. We also identify a requirement for porting MARL communication results to continuous control, that the decision a message informs must remain reachable by exploration, and show how to restore it.

</details>


### [71] [Steering Through Contact: A Finite-Support Motion Model for Single-Track Center-Articulated Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23271)
*Mohamed Dhia Ounally,Nicolas Samson,Mathis Turgeon-Roy,Veronica Vannini,Johann Laconte,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出有限支撑二次模型（FSQ），将接触阻力纳入单轨中铰接车辆的侧滑平衡，实现实时横向速度与偏航率预测，实测精度显著优于PCK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外机器人轨迹规划与控制需预测推进和转向对车辆运动的影响，尤其接触点发生滑移时。现有铰接车辆的点接触运动学模型（PCK）考虑连杆几何但忽略沿接触分布的旋转阻力，导致滑移预测不准确。

Method: 针对单轨中铰接车辆提出FSQ模型，通过侧滑的准静态平衡引入分布旋转阻力，放松点接触假设以推广PCK。将二次滑移代价精确化简为接触力矩，得到紧凑闭式解，用于实时预测横向速度和偏航率。

Result: 在沥青、草地、冰面和混合路线的真实实验中，使用超过7公里数据评估。对于5秒预测，FSQ相对PCK将加权中位平移误差和偏航误差分别降低53.5%和68.9%。

Conclusion: FSQ通过纳入分布接触阻力并给出闭式解，显著提升铰接车辆滑移下的运动预测精度，具备实时性，适用于野外机器人轨迹规划与控制。

Abstract: Trajectory planning and control in field robotics rely on predicting how propulsion and steering affect vehicle motion when contact points undergo slip. For articulated vehicles, the point-contact kinematic model (PCK) accounts for the linkage geometry but neglects the rotational resistance distributed along the contacts. We propose a finite-support quadratic model (FSQ) for single-track, center-articulated vehicles that incorporates this resistance through a quasi-static balance of lateral slip. Our approach generalizes the standard PCK formulation by relaxing the contact-point assumption. An exact reduction of the quadratic slip cost to contact moments gives a compact closed-form solution for real-time prediction of lateral velocity and yaw rate. We evaluate the proposed method in real-world experiments across asphalt, grass, ice, and mixed routes, using more than 7 km of data. For five-second predictions, FSQ reduces the weighted median translation and yaw errors by 53.5% and 68.9%, respectively, relative to PCK.

</details>
