Method: 构建Nuni床边机器人原型，将检测到的痛苦线索作为询问理由而非报警理由。对比两种X3D-UGT RGB外观分类器（在NTU RGB+D上六分类准确率达97.7%和94.8%）与一种以姿态为中心的混合流水线，后者在机器人摄像机拍摄的28段单演员脚本化视频上进行评估。另外通过事件注入测试"先询问"控制器。

Result: 混合流水线六分类宏召回率为0.71，而微调与从零训练的RGB变体分别为0.25和0.29。混合路径在16段痛苦片段中的12段产生触发提问的痛苦线索，在8段正常片段中仅2段会不必要地提问；RGB变体分别仅在2/16和3/16的痛苦片段中产生触发提问的线索。13次状态转换试验全部通过：有效回应导致取消报警，两次未应答提示产生一次报警，三种边界条件均正确处理。

Conclusion: 这些结果是初步技术评估，而非用户研究或医学验证，但表明交互策略可以限制不确定感知带来的后果，为机器人视角下的身体线索识别与交互设计提供了可行思路。

Abstract: Body-cue recognition can support assistive robots, but benchmark accuracy does not guarantee reliable behavior under a robot-camera viewpoint. We present Nuni, a bedside robot prototype that treats a detected distress cue as a reason to ask rather than a reason to alert. We compare two X3D-UGT RGB appearance classifiers, which reach 97.7% and 94.8% six-way accuracy on NTU RGB+D, with a pose-centric hybrid pipeline on 28 single-actor scripted clips recorded from the robot camera. The hybrid path achieved 0.71 six-way macro recall, versus 0.25 and 0.29 for the fine-tuned and from-scratch RGB variants. More importantly for interaction, it produced a question-triggering distress cue in 12/16 distress clips and would have prompted unnecessarily in 2/8 normal clips; the RGB variants yielded a question-triggering cue in only 2/16 and 3/16 distress clips. We separately tested the question-first controller through event injection. All 13 state-transition trials passed: valid responses caused stand-down, two unanswered prompts produced one alert, and three boundary conditions were handled correctly. These results are a preliminary technical evaluation, not a user study or medical validation, but they show how interaction policy can limit the consequences of uncertain perception.

</details>


### [108] [CARE: Experience-Guided Atomic Corrective Execution for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24118)
*Junlan Xiao,Junwei Jiang,Zaibin Zhang,Yifan Wang,Zhongbo Zhang,Huchuan Lu,Lijun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CARE框架，通过从执行失败中学习来提升VLA策略的恢复能力，并引入FSR-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在机器人操作中表现强，但一旦执行偏离标称轨迹就变得脆弱。

Method: 收集失败rollout，建模阶段条件的失败后偏差，用经验分布合成代表性失败状态与纠正示范；推理时结合阶段规划与物理3D监控触发原子调整或重操作。

Result: 在多种VLA骨干、仿真基准和真实双臂任务上一致提升，仿真平均任务成功率+14.5点，真实世界+15.9点。

Conclusion: 从失败中学习可有效增强VLA策略的恢复能力，并提供FSR-Bench评估基准。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies achieve strong performance in robotic manipulation but remain brittle once execution deviates from nominal trajectories. We propose CARE (Corrective Atomic Robotic Execution), a framework that improves recovery by learning from failures encountered during execution. Instead of generating corrective data from manually designed or random perturbations, CARE collects failed rollouts, models stage-conditioned post-failure deviations, and uses the resulting empirical distributions to synthesize representative failure states and corrective demonstrations. At inference time, CARE combines stage-wise planning with physically grounded 3D monitoring to trigger atomic adjustments or re-operations while preserving task progress. We further introduce the Failure State Recovery Benchmark (FSR-Bench), which evaluates recovery from intermediate failure states under local deviations and structural anomalies. Experiments across multiple VLA backbones, simulation benchmarks, and real-world dual-arm tasks show consistent improvements, with average task-success gains of 14.5 points in simulation and 15.9 points in the real world. Code, models, and data are available at https://github.com/xiaojunlan/care

</details>


### [109] [ActiveArena: Benchmarking and Understanding Active Perception in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24124)
*Yibo Li,Enshen Zhou,Rui Chen,Yanjun Ding,Mengzhen Liu,Yi Han,Jiabo Zhan,Lipeng Wang,Shanghang Zhang,Lu Sheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ActiveArena-Sim仿真器、ActiveArena-Bench基准（35个任务）及ActiveArena-VLA模块化配置套件，用于评测机器人的主动感知与操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以评估机器人如何在主动方式下有效获取并维护记忆中的信息，因此需要一个可控视角、大规模工作空间的主动感知测试平台。

Method: 构建ActiveArena-Sim仿真器；设计5大类35个任务的ActiveArena-Bench，提供记忆标注、标准化训练数据及ID/OOD协议；提出13种视觉-语言-动作配置的ActiveArena-VLA，用于研究记忆写入、容量、本体状态、子任务监督与高层规划。

Result: 基准结果显示明显的ID-OOD差距：均匀记忆采样、可靠写入策略下的更大记忆容量、本体输入和子任务监督能提升OOD泛化；规划器引导的记忆管理与决策仅用稀疏记忆即可接近最佳配置性能。

Conclusion: ActiveArena为主动感知与操作的模型开发与诊断提供了统一测试平台。

Abstract: Active perception and manipulation are crucial for robots to interact with complex scenes. Existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate how robots effectively acquire and maintain information in memory in an active manner. To this end, we introduce ActiveArena-Sim, an active-perception simulator with controllable viewpoints and large-scale workspaces as the foundation. Built on this, we propose ActiveArena-Bench, which comprises 35 tasks across 5 fine-grained categories, covering visual exploration and interactive information acquisition. Each task is difficult to solve from passive observations alone, requiring multi-round evidence acquisition and memory-based reasoning. The benchmark provides rich memory annotations, standardized training data, and ID/OOD protocols featuring disjoint scenes, unseen distractor configurations, and novel backgrounds. Moreover, we present ActiveArena-VLA, a modular suite of 13 vision-language-action configurations for controlled studies of memory writing, memory capacity, proprioceptive state, subtask supervision, and high-level planning in active perception. Benchmark results reveal a substantial ID-OOD gap: uniform memory sampling, increased memory capacity under reliable write policies, proprioceptive inputs, and subtask supervision improve OOD generalization, while planner-guided memory management and decision-making achieve performance close to the best-performing configuration using only sparse memory.
  ActiveArena thus provides a unified testbed to develop and diagnose models for active perception and manipulation.

</details>


### [110] [Phrase-Level Robotic Guqin Performance: Bimanual Motion Planning and Audio-Tactile Interaction Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24133)
*Zhen Wang,Zhiheng Chen,Tianyuan Bao,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出异构双臂机器人系统实现古琴乐句级自主演奏，将演奏建模为混合离散-连续执行问题，实现93.6%与96.8%的事件正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人与具身智能虽已能完成复杂操作任务，但乐器演奏仍是严峻基准：它要求无碰撞轨迹、精确接触时序、非对称双臂协调，以及在真实乐器上达到目标声学效果。古琴为七弦无品乐器，弦距仅毫米级、右手拨弦瞬时、左手按音需持续接触，操作难度极大，现有工作难以胜任。

Method: 构建物理异构双臂机器人系统。将古琴演奏形式化为混合离散-连续执行问题，设计分层规划框架，协调工作手指分配、构型连续性、避障以及跨连续音乐事件的紧耦合双手接触时序。系统融合视觉引导的乐器定位、基于触觉的按音接触监测，以及听觉反馈驱动的拨弦参数标定。

Result: 在25个事件构成的真实乐句实验中，系统在物理古琴上稳定执行散音与七徽泛音序列协同演奏，多次重复试验中事件正确率分别达到93.6%和96.8%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，所提分层规划与多模态感知框架可支撑高精度的乐句级自主古琴演奏，为机器人在精细接触时序与音质约束下的操作提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics and embodied intelligence have enabled robots to perform increasingly complex manipulation tasks. However, musical instrument performance remains a formidable benchmark, demanding not only collision-free trajectory execution but also precise contact timing, asymmetric bimanual coordination, and target acoustic outcomes on physical instruments. The guqin, a seven-string fretless zither, presents unique manipulation challenges due to its millimetric string spacing, transient right-hand plucking, and sustained left-hand harmonic contacts. In this work, we present a physical heterogeneous dual-arm robotic system for phrase-level autonomous guqin performance. We formulate guqin playing as a hybrid discrete--continuous execution problem and develop a hierarchical planning framework that coordinates working finger assignment, configuration continuity, obstacle avoidance, and tight bimanual contact schedules across consecutive musical events. The system integrates vision-guided instrument localization, tactile-based harmonic contact monitoring, and auditory feedback-informed plucking parameter calibration. Real-world experiments on a 25-event phrase demonstrate that the system reliably executes coordinated open-string and seventh-hui harmonic sequences on a physical guqin, achieving 93.6% and 96.8% event correctness across repeated trials.

</details>


### [111] [BayesianGS-SLAM: Uncertainty-Aware Neural Rendering SLAM via Probabilistic Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24140)
*Kyeongsu Kang,Seongbo Ha,Sibaek Lee,Hyeonwoo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BayesianGS-SLAM，一种不确定性感知的3D高斯泼溅SLAM框架，在映射中估计颜色与深度的预测不确定性，并将其统一复用于映射、跟踪与关键帧选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经渲染SLAM依赖渲染的RGB-D残差进行相机跟踪与地图优化，但受传感器噪声、观测覆盖不足及地图表示不完整影响，这些预测的可靠性差异较大。缺乏显式可靠性估计时，不可靠残差会损害位姿优化，而已被当前地图充分解释的帧又会触发冗余的映射更新。

Method: 采用可处理的概率建模，将传感器噪声不确定性分量与不透明度引起的地图表示不确定性分量相结合，并沿渲染过程进行传播，从而获得预测不确定性。该不确定性被用于增强映射、通过鲁棒位姿目标函数对跟踪残差进行归一化，以及基于预测惊喜度的关键帧判据来评估新帧。与以往仅考虑颜色不确定性或只在映射阶段使用不确定性的方法不同，本框架同时估计颜色与深度的预测不确定性并贯穿整个流程。

Result: 在真实RGB-D数据集上，深度不确定性—误差排序相较现有不确定性感知SLAM方法有显著提升；同时所提出的关键帧选择策略减少了关键帧数量与映射调用次数，并保持有竞争力的跟踪与渲染性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将颜色与深度的预测不确定性一致地集成到映射、跟踪和关键帧选择中，能够提升不确定性估计质量并提高SLAM流程的效率。

Abstract: Neural-rendering-based SLAM relies on rendered RGB-D residuals for camera tracking and map optimization, but the reliability of these predictions can vary substantially because of sensor noise, limited observation coverage, and incomplete map representations. Without an explicit reliability estimate, unreliable residuals may adversely affect pose optimization, while frames already well explained by the current map may trigger redundant mapping updates. In this paper, we present BayesianGS-SLAM, an uncertainty-aware 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM framework that estimates predictive color and depth uncertainty during mapping and consistently reuses it across the SLAM pipeline. Our tractable probabilistic formulation combines a sensor-noise uncertainty component with an opacity-induced map-representation component propagated through the rendering process. The resulting predictive uncertainty is used to augment mapping, normalize tracking residuals through a robust pose objective, and evaluate incoming frames using a predictive-surprise-based keyframe criterion. Unlike prior uncertainty-aware neural-rendering SLAM methods that primarily consider color uncertainty or use uncertainty only during mapping, our framework estimates predictive uncertainty for both color and depth and integrates it into mapping, tracking, and keyframe selection. Evaluations on real-world RGB-D datasets demonstrate substantially improved depth uncertainty-error ranking compared with existing uncertainty-aware SLAM methods. Moreover, the proposed keyframe-selection strategy reduces the number of selected keyframes and mapping calls while maintaining competitive tracking and rendering performance.

</details>


### [112] [MimicAgent: Quadruped Skills via Prompt-to-Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24145)
*Lucky Kant Nayak,Narayanan Palghat Parameswaran,Neehar Peri,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MimicAgent：一种面向四足机器人动态技能学习的"提示词到轨迹"生成框架，用编码智能体生成参考轨迹，替代传统奖励塑形，实现示例引导的强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人策略训练高度依赖奖励塑形，但奖励空间难以驾驭，需耗费大量"研究生式调参"时间。Eureka 虽尝试用 LLM 自动设计奖励，却难以跨技能与形态泛化。作者观察到：相比设计奖励函数，人类与 LLM 都更容易生成参考运动；而示例引导 RL 在类人机器人上已获成功，但四足机器人缺乏类似的大规模参考运动数据。

Method: 提出 MimicAgent，一个智能体式工具框架：给定技能提示词，由编码智能体生成四足参考轨迹；再用这些粗略参考轨迹训练示例引导的强化学习策略，并部署到仿真与真实世界。实验中使用 Claude Fable 5.1 在框架内进行提示。

Result: 在智能体框架内提示 Claude Fable 5.1 时，87% 的提示词能生成语义对齐的参考轨迹，且所得策略可部署于仿真与真实世界。

Conclusion: 以"生成参考运动"替代"塑形奖励函数"是一条更易泛化、更高效的路径，MimicAgent 为四足机器人动态技能学习提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: We present MimicAgent, a prompt-to-trajectory generation framework for learning dynamic quadruped skills. Although reward shaping is extensively used when training quadruped policies, navigating the resulting reward landscape is notoriously difficult, requiring hours of "graduate student descent". Eureka attempts to automate reward design with LLMs, but we find that it struggles to generalize across diverse skills and morphologies. Our key observation is that it is far easier for a human - and by association, an LLM - to generate reference motions than to shape reward functions. Our hypothesis is motivated by the success of example-guided RL for humanoids, which exploits large-scale motion capture datasets as references for training locomotion policies. Unlike humanoids, quadrupeds lack such reference motion data. Towards this end, we propose MimicAgent, an agentic harness that, given a skill prompt, generates quadruped reference trajectories with coding agents. These coarse reference trajectories are then used to train example-guided RL policies that are deployable in simulation and in the real-world. Notably, we find that when prompting Claude Fable 5.1 within our agentic harness, 87% of prompts yield semantically aligned reference trajectories.

</details>


### [113] [Object-Centric Conditioning for Visuomotor Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24155)
*Jijie Li,Xu Yang,Junhong Zou,Chunhai Zhao,Chaoyang Zhao,Zhen Lei,Xiangyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SlotFlow，一种以物体为中心的流匹配策略，通过解耦"是什么"语义与"在哪里"空间线索，提升视觉运动操作在空间分布外偏移和视觉干扰下的鲁棒性，同时保持低步推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人视觉运动策略包括自回归、扩散和流匹配模型。其中 Action-to-Action (A2A) 流匹配以历史动作先验代替随机噪声初始化，提高了推理效率，但陈旧的历史运动模式与纠缠的全局视觉表征会共同削弱模型在空间 OOD 偏移和视觉干扰下的鲁棒性。

Method: SlotFlow 将以物体为中心的表征引入流匹配策略：将场景观测解耦为语义（"what"）特征和轻量级图像平面空间（"where"）线索。语义表征用于提供物体感知的策略条件并抑制无关背景相关性；空间线索用于当前状态定位，改善对物体配置偏移的适应能力。

Result: 大量仿真与真实世界实验表明，该方法在视觉干扰和严重空间扰动下鲁棒性提升，同时保持 A2A 的低步数推理效率。受控初始化与感知消融实验进一步表明，物体中心化的定位是性能提升的主要来源，且它与有用的历史运动先验是互补而非替代关系。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心的感知定位可显著增强流匹配视觉运动策略在空间 OOD 和干扰下的鲁棒性，并与历史动作先验形成互补，为高效且鲁棒的操作策略提供了新方向。

Abstract: Robot visuomotor policies are commonly formulated as autoregressive, diffusion-based, or more recently, flow matching models. Among them, Action-to-Action (A2A) flow matching improves inference efficiency by initializing generation from historical action priors rather than stochastic noise. However, stale historical motion patterns and entangled global visual representations can jointly reduce robustness under spatial out-of-distribution (OOD) shifts and visual distractors. In this work, we propose SlotFlow, an object-centric flow matching policy for robust visuomotor manipulation. SlotFlow decouples scene observations into semantic ("what") features and lightweight image-plane spatial ("where") cues to provide object-aware policy conditioning and current-state grounding. The semantic representation suppresses irrelevant background correlations, while the spatial cue improves adaptation to shifted object configurations. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate improved robustness under visual distractors and severe spatial perturbations while preserving the low-step inference efficiency of A2A. Controlled initialization and perception ablations further identify object-centric grounding as a major source of the gains and show that it complements, rather than replaces, useful historical motion priors.

</details>


### [114] [GraspTune: Tactile-Driven Execution Refinement for Robust Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24180)
*Juntao Li,Xingke Xia,Sichao Liu,Daqiang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraspTune是一个触觉驱动的执行阶段精修框架，将视觉抓取提议转化为稳定物理抓取，在仿真与真机上大幅提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉抓取提议生成进展迅速，但如何将选定的提议转化为稳定的物理抓取，仍是执行阶段的核心挑战。

Method: 从名义提议出发，在接近、接触形成和最终执行阶段施加有界残余TCP运动；利用局部深度、触觉信号、状态和历史学习面向控制的接触语义，采用状态条件专家接触查询与多任务监督（接触变化、接触风险、闭合后就绪度）；该表征条件化一个扩散预训练的残余策略，并与PPO对齐实现闭环执行。

Result: 在20个物体类别、超60,000次仿真执行中，对四种提议生成器（GraspNet、Contact-GraspNet、AnyGrasp、VGN）分别提升稳定抓取成功率+19.22、+9.55、+12.45、+20.70个百分点；四折留出类别研究将未见物体执行率从54.58%提升至70.33%；在UR5e配Xense指尖传感器的真机上，超1,000次试验将GraspNet执行率从71.0%提升至84.3%，且无需真实世界策略微调。

Conclusion: GraspTune将视觉上合理的提议转化为稳定的物理抓取，可服务于下游接触丰富操作任务，并具备类别不相关泛化与直接的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Visual grasp proposal generation has advanced rapidly, yet converting a selected proposal into a stable physical grasp remains a central execution-stage challenge. This paper introduces GraspTune, a tactile-driven execution-stage refinement framework that starts from a nominal proposal and applies bounded residual TCP motions during approach, contact formation, and final grasp execution. GraspTune learns control-facing contact semantics from local depth, tactile signals, state, and history using state-conditioned expert contact queries and multi-task supervision for contact change, contact risk, and post-close readiness. The representation conditions a diffusion-pretrained residual policy and is aligned with PPO for closed-loop execution. Across more than 60,000 simulated executions over 20 object categories, GraspTune establishes an execution-layer benefit across four proposal generators, raising stable grasp success by +19.22, +9.55, +12.45, and +20.70 percentage points for GraspNet, Contact-GraspNet, AnyGrasp, and VGN. A four-fold held-out category study raises unseen-object execution from 54.58% to 70.33%, showing category-disjoint generalization of contact correction. Across more than 1,000 real-robot trials on a UR5e setup with Xense fingertip sensors, GraspTune raises GraspNet execution from 71.0% to 84.3%, validating direct transfer without realworld policy fine-tuning. Together, these results turn visually plausible proposals into stable physical grasps for downstream contact-rich manipulation. A supplementary video is available at https://youtu.be/kcq7fSLNtzU.

</details>


### [115] [StenoVLA-3D: 3D-Aware Reasoning VLA for Navigation Through Gastrointestinal Stenoses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24187)
*Tamima Tabassum,Yiming Huang,Tianchun Wu,Changjing Liu,Zhiqing Tang,Chikit Ng,Beilei Cui,Liangjing Shao,Jiewen Lai,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StenoVLA-3D是一个面向狭窄区域自主内镜导航的3D感知VLA框架，通过几何门控融合点云地图与时间状态分支，实现了安全控制与病灶报告生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主内镜导航要求模型能从纹理稀疏的单目观测中预测动作、做出安全控制决策，并在病灶离开视野后保留证据。现有VLA模型主要依赖视觉外观和短期上下文，缺乏几何基础与整段手术的报告能力。

Method: 提出StenoVLA-3D框架：通过可学习的几何门控融合将点云地图（point-maps）集成到Cosmos-Reason 2主干网络；设计时间状态分支建模穿越进度；推理与动作主干同时输出有依据的推理与动作，专用头估计狭窄形状并生成最终病灶报告。同时构建EndoCausal数据集，包含病灶标注、动作和时间有据推理。

Result: 在40个保留的录制测试片段上，语义准确率95.2%、动作准确率83.4%；在物理3自由度内镜上，食管和结肠体模任务成功率分别为88.9%和77.8%（各36次试验），显著优于评估基线。

Conclusion: 将3D几何信息与时间状态建模引入VLA框架，可提升内镜狭窄区域导航的语义理解、动作决策与病灶报告能力，并在物理平台上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous endoscopic navigation requires the policy model to predict actions from texture-poor monocular observations, make safe control decisions, and retain evidence of lesions after they leave the field of view. Existing vision-language-action (VLA) models primarily rely on visual appearance and short-term context, limiting geometric grounding and episode-level reporting. We introduce StenoVLA-3D, a 3D-aware VLA framework for navigating through stenotic regions. We integrate point-maps into the Cosmos-Reason 2 backbone through learned geometry-gated fusion, and also propose a temporal state branch to model traversal progress. Our reasoning-and-action backbone predicts grounded reasoning with actions, while dedicated heads estimate stenosis shape and generate the final lesion report. We further introduce EndoCausal, an episode-level dataset with lesion annotations, actions, and temporally grounded reasoning. On 40 held-out recorded test episodes, StenoVLA-3D reaches 95.2\% semantic accuracy and 83.4\% action accuracy. On the physical 3-DoF endoscope, it attains 88.9\% and 77.8\% task success in esophageal and colonic phantoms (36 trials each), substantially outperforming the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [116] [A Topological Representation with Object-Path Graphs for Open-Vocabulary Instance Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24189)
*Linwei Zheng,Daojie Peng,Bingtao Wang,Haoang Li,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出对象-路径图（object-path graph），将开放词汇语义推理与拓扑导航统一在单一轻量拓扑框架中，无需密集度量地图即可实现视觉语言导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法要么将导航分解为顺序的语言引导决策，要么在缺乏先验环境知识的情况下依赖在线探索。场景图表示虽提供紧凑语义记忆，但与下游导航解耦，导航仍依赖密集度量地图。因此需要一个能统一语义推理与拓扑导航的表示。

Method: 提出对象-路径图表示，联合支持语义定位、基于图的定位和导航，集成于单一轻量拓扑框架。基于该图提出导航策略：结合全局路径规划与局部节点间执行，通过轻量节点定位和语义视觉伺服实现，直接在图上导航而无需密集度量重建。

Result: 在 HM3D 和 Replica 数据集上，通过所提出的分层图结构在开放词汇物体定位上取得有竞争力的性能，同时实现有效的导航表现。真实机器人实验进一步验证了框架的实用性。

Conclusion: 对象-路径图有效弥合了场景图语义记忆与导航之间的差距，实现了无需密集度量地图的开放词汇语义导航，并在仿真与真实环境中验证了实用性。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation requires embodied agents to navigate environments using natural language instructions and visual observations. Existing approaches typically decompose navigation into sequential language-guided decisions or rely on online exploration without prior environmental knowledge. Scene graph representations offer compact semantic memory but remain decoupled from downstream navigation, which still depends on dense metric maps. To close this gap, we propose an object--path graph that unifies open-vocabulary semantic reasoning with topological navigation. The proposed representation jointly supports semantic grounding, graph-based localization, and navigation within a single lightweight topological framework. Building on this graph, we introduce a navigation strategy that combines global path planning with local inter-node execution through lightweight node localization and semantic visual servoing, enabling navigation directly over the graph without dense metric reconstruction. Experiments on HM3D and Replica demonstrate competitive performance in open-vocabulary object grounding through the proposed hierarchical graph structure, while achieving effective navigation performance. Real-world robot experiments further validate the practicality of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [117] [Odometry-Aided Real-Time Mapping for Underwater Robots Using Forward-Looking Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24195)
*Siyuan Du,Kanzhong Yao,Youdong Wang,Yingqi Liu,Qingwen Liu,Qunhui Yang,Zhe Sun,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出级联特征重建流程与姿态感知几何投影，实现水下前视声纳实时2.5D建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下复杂环境中光衰减与散射严重影响光学感知，前视声纳可在弱光下提供高频声学成像，但实时建图受目标高程未解析、空间非均匀噪声和边界破碎等问题制约。

Method: 采用级联特征重建流程：基于FFT去噪、快速多尺度恒虚警率检测与梯度自适应边界连接提取几何特征；结合姿态感知几何投影与增量占据栅格累积构建深度参考的2.5D地图；声纳垂直位置由外部传感器提供，目标高程基于显式几何假设赋值。

Result: 在3 m × 5 m水池实验中，三条序列的平面建图精度达厘米级，RMSE低于3 cm，平均每帧处理时间为42.4 ms。

Conclusion: 该流程可在受限水下环境中以低延迟实现高精度实时前视声纳2.5D建图。

Abstract: Reliable perception is essential for underwater vehicles operating in complex environments, where light attenuation and scattering often degrade visibility and compromise optical sensing. Forward-looking sonar (FLS) offers an alternative by providing high-frame-rate acoustic imaging under poor optical conditions. However, real-time FLS mapping remains challenging due to unresolved target elevation, spatially non-uniform noise, and fragmented target boundaries, which hinder feature extraction and introduce geometric ambiguity during projection. To address these challenges, we propose a cascaded feature reconstruction pipeline combining fast Fourier transform (FFT)-based denoising, fast multiscale constant false alarm rate (MCFAR) detection, and gradient-adaptive boundary connection to extract geometric features from degraded sonar images with low latency. We integrate attitude-aware geometric projection with incremental occupancy accumulation to construct a depth-referenced 2.5D map for local mapping in confined underwater environments. The sonar's vertical position is referenced to an external sensor, while target elevation is assigned under an explicit geometric assumption rather than measured directly by FLS. Experiments in a 3 m X 5 m pool demonstrate centimeter-scale planar mapping accuracy, with a root-mean-square error (RMSE) below 3 cm across three sequences and an average processing time of 42.4 ms per frame.

</details>


### [118] [Audio-based UAV Localization with Adaptive Temporal Correspondence via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24218)
*Haoxiang Lei,Mingzheng Feng,Daotong Wang,Shenghai Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于自适应时间对应的音频定位框架，用探测片段提取声学状态，强化学习控制器动态决定音频窗口大小，再由Mamba定位网络进行三维定位，在保持精度同时大幅降低时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频定位方法依赖预设固定长度的音频片段，限制了时间对应关系，并在"足够声学证据"与"及时定位"之间形成权衡。

Method: 先用探测片段提取紧凑的声学状态，表征观测的可靠性与一致性；由强化学习控制器依据该状态动态确定每次定位决策所需的音频窗口大小；将选定的音频片段输入基于Mamba的定位网络，并通过自适应时间特征调制进行三维位置估计。

Result: 大量实验表明，与SOTA方法相比，本方法在实现有竞争力的三维定位精度的同时，显著降低了时间对应时延，并在多种场景下展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 自适应时间对应可有效缓解固定窗口带来的精度—时延权衡问题，为反无人机预警提供了一种兼顾准确性与实时性的音频定位方案。

Abstract: Audio-based localization provides a low-cost and illumination-independent sensing solution for anti-UAV early warning. However, existing methods typically rely on a predefined fixed audio segment length, which limits temporal correspondence and creates a trade-off between sufficient acoustic evidence and timely localization. To address this issue, we propose an audio-based localization framework with adaptive temporal correspondence. A probe segment is first used to extract a compact acoustic state that characterizes the reliability and consistency of the observation. Guided by the state, a reinforcement learning controller dynamically determines the required audio window size for each localization decision. The selected audio segment is then processed by a Mamba-based localization network with adaptive temporal feature modulation for 3D position estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive 3D localization accuracy with substantially reduced temporal correspondence latency compared to SOTA methods and exhibits strong generalization across scenarios.

</details>


### [119] [OpenFlyScan: A Quality-Guided Aerial Reconstruction System for Consumer Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24253)
*Zhongrui You,Zhen Li,Junli Liu,Zhigang Wang,Bin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenFlyScan：面向消费级无人机的质量引导航拍重建系统，结合GS质量模型、重采集规划器与移动App，实现快速低成本城市资产创建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS可提供高保真大规模具身仿真场景，但大规模城市资产构建受限于昂贵设备和滞后质量反馈；预设航测可能遗漏复杂表面，缺陷仅在重建后发现，需返工重采，成本高。

Method: 系统包含三部分：(1) GS质量模型，从GS渲染误差中学习，预测区域重建质量；(2) 重采集规划器，基于质量预测生成互补的航拍条带；(3) 定制移动App，支持自动化倾斜航测与数据传输，无需额外机载硬件。

Result: 在真实航拍场景中，质量模型能有效识别可能重建较差的区域；在Expo West实地实验中，针对性重采集使新增视角的PSNR提升10.95 dB。

Conclusion: OpenFlyScan在消费级无人机上整合采集、针对性重采集与重建，支持快速、低成本的城市资产创建，代码与模型将公开。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides high-fidelity scenes for large-scale embodied simulation, but constructing large-scale urban assets remains constrained by expensive equipment and delayed quality feedback. Preset surveys can leave complex surfaces insufficiently observed, with defects discovered only after reconstruction, requiring return visits and repeated processing. We present OpenFlyScan, a quality-guided aerial reconstruction system for consumer drones that integrates a GS quality model, a reacquisition planner, and a custom-designed mobile app. The model learns from GS rendering errors to predict regional reconstruction quality. Based on these predictions, the planner then generates complementary reacquisition strips to be executed through the app, which also supports automated oblique surveys and data transfer without additional hardware on board. Across real aerial scenes, the model effectively identifies regions that are likely to be poorly reconstructed. In the Expo West field experiment, targeted reacquisition improves PSNR at additional views by 10.95 dB. With consumer drones, OpenFlyScan integrates capture, targeted reacquisition, and reconstruction to support rapid, low-cost urban asset creation. Code and models will be made publicly available at https://openflyscan.github.io/.

</details>


### [120] [ME-Brain-1.0: Memory, Cognition and Action for Evolving Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24271)
*Wei He,Hengtao Li,Zhongrui Yu,Xuhan Zhu,Maokui He,Zide Liu,Xiyue Zhang,Xianwei Mao,Chunpeng Zhou,Jia Shi,Yanze Xin,Jingwen Li,Jingxie Zheng,Sijie Zeng,Chenfeng Wang,Fan Lu,Zeyu Zhang,Shuai Guo,Hengxuan Zhang,Pengfei Yu,Jia Shi,Yu Liu,Kun Zhan,Yan Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ME-Brain 是一种可自演化的具身系统，通过"动作执行—经验获取—经验演化—改进执行"闭环，使具身智能从"训练后冻结"转向"部署后持续演化"，且无需重新训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身系统依赖预训练能力，部署后能力固定，难以从物理交互中持续学习，限制了自身的进化与适应能力。

Method: 提出 MachEmbodied-Brain（ME-Brain）闭环架构，包含三个核心模块：Evolvable Memory 将多模态轨迹整合为分层、可复用的经验；Cognitive Core 将物理经验转化为可迁移技能；Action Model 结合事件驱动关键帧、EventCell 局部世界预测与动作条件化记忆调制，把计算聚焦于决策关键的时刻、区域与历史证据。

Result: Cognitive Core 在具身与智能体基准上分别领先最强对比模型 8.2 和 9.6 分；Action Model 在 RoboMME 上取得 47.88% 平均成功率，提升 3.26 分；RoboDojo 上平均 Score 21.51、成功率 16.03%，分别超过 π₀.₅ 10.10 和 9.12 分；六任务 ME-RealBench 上平均 Score 69.5、成功率 66.7%，分别超过 DM0.5 12.8 和 11.7 分。

Conclusion: ME-Brain 通过闭环自演化机制实现了部署后持续进化，在多个具身与智能体基准上显著超越强基线，验证了"deploy-and-evolve"范式的有效性。

Abstract: Current embodied systems largely rely on pretrained capabilities that remain fixed after deployment, limiting their ability to learn from physical interaction. We introduce MachEmbodied-Brain (ME-Brain), a self-evolving embodied system organized around a closed loop of action execution, experience acquisition, experience evolution, and improved execution. Evolvable Memory consolidates multimodal trajectories into hierarchical, reusable experience; Cognitive Core transforms physical experience into transferable skills; and the Action Model combines event-driven keyframes, EventCell local-world prediction, and action-conditioned memory modulation to focus computation on decision-critical moments, regions, and historical evidence. Together, these modules shift embodied intelligence from train-and-freeze to deploy-and-evolve without model retraining. Cognitive Core outperforms the strongest comparison models by 8.2 and 9.6 points on embodied and agent benchmarks. The Action Model achieves 47.88% mean success on RoboMME, a 3.26-point improvement over the strongest baseline. On RoboDojo, it reaches a 21.51 mean Score and 16.03% success rate, exceeding $π_{0.5}$ by 10.10 and 9.12 points. On the six-task ME-RealBench, ME-Brain achieves a 69.5 mean Score and 66.7% success rate, outperforming DM0.5 by 12.8 and 11.7 points, respectively.

</details>


### [121] [vla.simd: Efficient CPU Inference for Language-Conditioned Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24274)
*Khanh D. Nguyen,Hoang M. Truong,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: vla.simd是一个面向CPU的VLA推理引擎，结合专用策略IMPACT，在无GPU条件下实现高效语言条件操作部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在没有专用GPU的情况下部署语言条件操作，需要高效推理以及能覆盖策略查询间隔的动作块；现有方案缺乏对CPU部署中查询延迟与动作供给关系的系统分析与优化。

Method: 提出vla.simd：融合共享SIMD微内核、可复用计算和目标特定优化；将查询延迟与执行时域关联到滞后及时间对齐执行下的动作可用性，区分动作供给与反馈频率；并引入IMPACT策略，基于ACT，采用缓存文本表示和语言调制视觉特征。评测覆盖六种策略与四款CPU。

Result: 相比编译版PyTorch参考实现，中位加速约1.4倍且保持fp32数值保真；IMPACT是评测集中唯一在Raspberry Pi 5上提供至少30 actions/s的语言条件策略，90秒热稳定后fp32达33.5 actions/s、int8达81.2；GPU评测在四个LIBERO套件上无机器人预训练取得76.4%平均成功率；指令打乱测试显示能在熟悉目标间选择；在SO-101机械臂（IMPACT）和UR10e+Robotiq夹爪（SmolVLA）上验证了两种机器人本体的CPU部署。

Conclusion: 研究表明通过CPU推理引擎与高效策略设计，可在无GPU条件下实现语言条件操作的有效部署，并在多策略、多CPU及多种机器人本体上得到验证。

Abstract: Deploying language-conditioned manipulation without a dedicated GPU requires efficient inference and action chunks that cover the delay between policy queries. We present vla.simd, a CPU inference engine that combines shared SIMD micro-kernels, reusable computation, and target-specific optimization. We relate query latency and execution horizon to action availability under lagged and time-aligned execution, distinguishing action supply from feedback frequency. Across six policies and four CPUs, vla.simd achieves approximately $1.4\times$ median speedup over compiled PyTorch references while preserving fp32 numerical fidelity. We also introduce IMPACT, an ACT-based policy with cached text representations and language-modulated visual features. IMPACT is the only language-conditioned policy in our evaluated set that supplies at least 30 actions/s on the Raspberry Pi 5: after a 90 s thermal soak, it supplies 33.5 actions/s in fp32 and 81.2 with int8. Separate GPU evaluations yield $76.4\%$ mean success across four LIBERO suites without robot pretraining; instruction-shuffling tests demonstrate selection among familiar goals. Trials with IMPACT on an SO-101 arm and SmolVLA on a UR10e with a Robotiq gripper demonstrate CPU deployment on two robot embodiments.

</details>


### [122] [Performance-Preserving Online Adaptation in Social Navigation via Diffusion Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24317)
*Haruto Nagahisa,Kohei Matsumoto,Yuki Hyodo,Ryo Kurazume*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DSRL：保持扩散策略固定、仅训练噪声策略，并结合多种子扩散策略集成构建基础策略，实现社交导航中保留性能的高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交导航需建模人机复杂交互，深度强化学习被广泛研究。但仿真无法完整复现多样化场景、机器人动力学及部署环境中不同的社会规范，故需在部署环境中微调；同时必须保留基础模型性能，以免损害"避让行人并到达目的地"这一主要导航目标。

Method: 提出 DSRL（diffusion steering via reinforcement learning），通过强化学习对噪声策略进行扩散引导，固定扩散策略仅训练噪声策略；并将多种子训练的扩散式 RL 策略集成为基础策略。

Result: 与其它方法相比，所提方法在保留性能的同时实现高效学习；并通过适应社会规范验证了灵活的行为控制，且经硬件在环仿真证明了在真实机器人上的有效性。

Conclusion: DSRL 可在不牺牲导航主要目标的前提下实现高效微调与灵活行为控制，具备向物理机器人迁移的可行性。

Abstract: In social navigation, modeling the complex interactions between humans and robots is difficult, and deep reinforcement learning has therefore been actively studied. However, because simulation alone cannot fully reproduce diverse scenarios, robot dynamics, and the social conventions that vary across deployment environments, fine-tuning in the deployment environment is promising. In doing so, learning that preserves the base model's performance is required, so as not to compromise the primary objective of navigation, namely avoiding pedestrians and reaching the destination. In this study, we propose a method that applies diffusion steering via reinforcement learning (DSRL), which trains only the noise policy while keeping the diffusion policy fixed, thereby achieving learning that preserves performance. Furthermore, we integrate diffusion-based RL policies trained with multiple seeds to construct the base policy, improving learning performance. Our evaluation shows that, compared with other methods, the proposed method enables efficient learning while preserving performance, and we confirm flexible behavior control through adaptation to social conventions, as well as its effectiveness on a physical robot through hardware-in-the-loop simulation.

</details>


### [123] [LIBERO-VPro: Benchmarking Closed-Loop Visual Robustness of Robotic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24350)
*Huiqiong Li,Zhiting Mei,Anirudha Majumdar,Jingjing Chen,Yu-Gang Jiang,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LIBERO-VPro 基准，系统评测机器人基础模型在闭环执行中的视觉鲁棒性，发现名义性能强的模型在视觉退化下存在显著且架构相关的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型虽在标准操作基准上表现优异，但这些评测通常假设执行过程中视觉观测干净、及时且一致，无法反映真实场景中视觉证据受扰动时的闭环鲁棒性。因此需要一个系统化诊断框架来评测模型在挑战性视觉条件下的动作落地与适应能力。

Method: 构建 LIBERO-VPro 基准，通过扰动执行时可获得的视觉证据，从四个互补维度评测闭环视觉鲁棒性：视觉证据退化、相机陈旧、视觉来源一致性、任务相关场景变化。基准涵盖 12 类挑战、96 种实验设置、3,296 个任务条件用例。评测 3 个视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型与 3 个世界-动作模型（WAM），约 196,000 次仿真回合，并在 Franka Research 3 上补充 200 次真机实验。

Result: 强名义性能可能掩盖视觉落地与适应方面的实质弱点。模型在严重物体级遮挡下仍常成功，但一旦局部交互线索被破坏或熟悉的空间先验被违反，性能急剧下降；对陈旧或缺失观测高度敏感；当任务前提改变需要行为适应时表现困难。VLA 与 WAM 呈现不同的鲁棒性画像，说明视觉鲁棒性是多维且依赖架构的。

Conclusion: LIBERO-VPro 提供了一个系统化诊断框架，用于推动机器人基础模型在挑战性视觉条件下更可靠地进行动作落地与适应。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models achieve impressive performance on standard manipulation benchmarks, yet these evaluations typically assume clean, timely, and consistent visual observations throughout execution. We introduce LIBERO-VPro, a benchmark for systematically evaluating the closed-loop visual robustness of robotic foundation models by perturbing the visual evidence available during execution. LIBERO-VPro covers four complementary dimensions, including Visual Evidence Degradation, Camera Staleness, Visual Source Consistency, and Task-Relevant Scene Variation, spanning 12 challenge categories, 96 experimental settings, and 3,296 task-condition cases. We evaluate three vision-language-action models and three world-action models over approximately 196,000 simulated episodes, complemented by 200 real-world rollouts on a Franka Research 3. Our results reveal that strong nominal performance can mask substantial weaknesses in visual grounding and adaptation. Models often remain successful despite severe object-level occlusion, yet degrade sharply when local interaction cues are disrupted or familiar spatial priors are violated. They are also highly sensitive to stale or missing observations and struggle when changed task preconditions require behavioral adaptation. Finally, VLAs and WAMs exhibit distinct robustness profiles, showing that visual robustness is multi-dimensional and architecture-dependent. LIBERO-VPro provides a systematic diagnostic framework for developing robotic foundation models that can more reliably ground and adapt their actions under challenging visual conditions.

</details>


### [124] [Multi-Agent Transportation of Free-Flyers in Microgravity Via Pushing Interaction Under Human-in-the-Loop Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24376)
*Gregorio Marchesini,Nicola De Carli,Sihyun Cho,Youngkyoung Kong,Elias Krantz,Mani Hemanth Dhullipalla,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出微重力下多追捕机器人通过单侧推动接触协同运输被动目标的框架，结合CLF-CBF与混合整数推力分配，实现延迟感知反馈控制，保证目标稳定与安全避障，并通过Gazebo仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力环境中被动目标的协同运输需求，且追捕者只能通过单侧推动接触作用，导致执行约束稀疏且依赖构型，需在期望推力分配变化时物理重定位，控制权间歇，稳定性与安全性挑战大。

Method: 构建延迟感知反馈控制架构：在混合整数推力分配程序中引入控制李雅普诺夫函数（CLF）约束保证稳定性，控制障碍函数（CBF）约束保证安全性，以跟踪人类给定的期望运动旋量并满足单侧推动约束与重定位需求。

Result: 实现参考跟踪的同时，在控制权间歇条件下保证对圆形障碍物的避障安全；通过Gazebo仿真验证了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为太空环境下自由飞行器的人机监督协同运输提供了基础，展示CLF-CBF与混合整数分配结合在推力受限、间歇控制场景中的可行性与安全性。

Abstract: We propose a safety-critical framework for the cooperative transportation of passive targets in microgravity, where a team of chaser robots acts through unilateral pushing contacts to track a human-provided desired twist while ensuring safe target motion. The pushing-only nature of the interaction introduces sparse, configuration-dependent actuation constraints requiring chasers to physically relocate on the target body when the desired pushing allocation changes. To address these challenges, we formulate a delay-aware feedback control architecture leveraging Control Lyapunov Function (CLF) and Control Barrier Function (CBF) constraints within a mixed-integer thrust allocation program to enforce stability and safety of the target, respectively. The proposed framework enables reference tracking while guaranteeing obstacle avoidance with a circular obstacle despite intermittent control authority, providing a foundation for human-supervised cooperative transportation of free-flyers in space environments. The proposed framework is validated through Gazebo simulations.

</details>


### [125] [Tactile-JEPA: Topology-Aware Self-Supervised Representation Learning for Distributed Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24385)
*Elizaveta Kovtun,Matvey Konovalov,Andrey Sakhovskiy,Semen Budennyy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Tactile-JEPA，一种基于传感器空间布局的自监督预训练方法，用于稀疏分布式电子皮肤，学习拓扑感知的触觉表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉感知对机器人接触丰富的灵巧操作至关重要，尤其在视觉遮挡下。图像编码器通常预训练，而触觉编码器多从原始噪声信号从零训练，限制了表达能力。现有自监督方法主要针对视觉触觉传感器，忽略分布式电子皮肤，且其传感元件稀疏、不规则排列，无法直接套用视觉SSL方法。

Method: Tactile-JEPA利用传感器空间布局进行自监督预训练：通过传感器连接图指导空间掩码，从可见元件预测被掩码元件的嵌入。采用双尺度掩码以同时捕获局部接触细节和全局表面状态。

Result: 在磁性和压阻传感器、不同机器人构型、单/双传感器配置的三个数据集上，力估计误差降低6.3%，手内方向误差降低20.8%，并在策略学习等下游任务中取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 触觉感知的收益关键取决于编码器预训练质量，Tactile-JEPA直接解决了该问题，验证了拓扑感知自监督预训练的有效性。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is an essential modality for robots performing contact-rich, dexterous manipulation, particularly under visual occlusion. While pre-trained image encoders are standard in robot learning pipelines, tactile encoders are still commonly trained from scratch from raw, noisy signals, which might limit their expressivity. Existing self-supervised learning (SSL) approaches focus predominantly on vision-based tactile sensors, leaving distributed electronic skins largely unaddressed. These sensors, however, have a distinctive property: their sensing elements are sparse and irregularly arranged over the surface they cover, which makes direct reuse of visual SSL methods suboptimal. We present Tactile-JEPA, an efficient self-supervised pre-training method that uses the spatial arrangement of tactile sensors to learn topology-aware representations. Specifically, it is trained to predict the embeddings of masked sensing elements from the unmasked remainder, using the sensor connectivity graph to guide spatial masking. Our analysis shows that effective tactile representations require capturing both local contact details and the global state of the tactile surface, which we achieve through dual-scale masking. Across three diverse datasets spanning magnetic and piezoresistive sensors, different robot embodiments, and single- and paired-sensor configurations, Tactile-JEPA reduces force estimation error by 6.3% and in-hand orientation error by 20.8% over the prior state-of-the-art, with consistent gains in other downstream applications, including policy learning. Overall, our results demonstrate that the benefit of tactile sensing depends critically on the quality of encoder pre-training, a problem which Tactile-JEPA addresses directly. Code is available at https://github.com/E-Kovtun/tactile.

</details>


### [126] [Zeva-Ego: Egocentric Mid-Training with In-Context Causal Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24411)
*Bingjia Huang,Xin Ding,Fu Chen,Kun Li,Wei Sun,Hao Wu,Yunxin Liu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Zeva-Ego框架，从第一视角人类视频中学习物理先验，并通过机器人交互持续进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角视频是物理交互经验的可扩展来源，但将其转化为机器人可执行知识并实现持续适应仍具挑战。

Method: 包含两个组件：动作中心编码器（ACE）将第一视角视觉转换转为以动作为中心的监督用于VLA中期训练；上下文因果学习（ICCL）在部署时基于动作-效果反馈实现无参数适应。

Result: Ego数据扩展至1万小时，RoboTwin成功率从63.8%提升至75.3%，接近2千小时机器人演示的74.7%，经验数据比约4-5:1；ICCL在四次尝试内将成功率从58%提升至89%，无需参数更新。

Conclusion: 展示了从人类经验学习并借助自身交互持续改进的可扩展具身智能路径。

Abstract: Egocentric video offers a scalable source of physical interaction experience, yet translating it into robot-executable knowledge and enabling continual adaptation remain challenging. We introduce Zeva-Ego, a unified framework that learns physical priors from human experience and evolves through robot interaction. An Action-Centric Encoder (ACE) converts egocentric visual transitions into action-centered supervision for VLA mid-training, while In-Context Causal Learning (ICCL) enables parameter-free adaptation from action-effect feedback at deployment. Scaling Ego data to 10K hours improves RoboTwin success from 63.8% to 75.3%, matching 2K hours of robot demonstrations (74.7%), corresponding to an empirical data ratio of roughly 4-5:1. With accumulated interaction experience, ICCL further improves success from 58% to 89% within four attempts without parameter updates. These results demonstrate a scalable path toward embodied intelligence that learns from human experience and continuously improves through its own interaction.

</details>


### [127] [Robotic Valve Turning: Axial Misalignment Correction Using Reaction Torque Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24413)
*Amit Kumar,Sri Harsha Turlapati,Gautami Golani,Yang Lin,Ravi N. Banavar,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用反作用力矩进行触觉更新控制的方法，用于校正机器人阀门操作中的轴向错位，并验证其稳定性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉估计容易受标定误差、遮挡和接触几何不确定性影响，而反作用力矩直接来自夹爪与阀门的物理交互，存在与错位误差向量的几何关系，可支撑更稳定的控制。

Method: 提出一种利用反作用力矩的触觉更新控制律，基于错位向量与反作用力矩之间的几何关系设计控制器，并证明其一致渐近稳定性；通过仿真验证，并使用Kinova Gen3机械臂在3个阀门位置、初始错位-15°至15°范围内进行实验测试。

Result: 控制律被证明为一致渐近稳定；仿真完成验证；18个测试案例中，错位中位数的绝对值在2.46°以内，反作用力矩中位数绝对值在0.23Nm以内。

Conclusion: 利用反作用力矩的触觉更新控制可有效校正阀门操作中的轴向错位，并展现良好的稳定性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a haptic update control law that uses reaction torques to correct axial misalignment during robotic valve manipulation. Unlike vision-based estimates, which can be affected by calibration errors, occlusion, and uncertainty in the contact geometry, reaction torques arise directly from the physical interaction between the gripper and valve. A geometric relationship exists between the error (misalignment) vector and these torques. The primary aim of this work is to propose a stable controller exploiting this geometric property. Our control law is proven to be uniformly asymptotically stable. Simulations are performed for verification. Furthermore, we experimentally test the robustness of our method using a Kinova Gen3 robotic arm for initial misalignments ranging from $-15^\circ$ to $15^\circ$ at 3 different valve positions and report the resulting data distribution. The absolute value of the median misalignment across all 18 test cases is found to be within $2.46^\circ$ and that of reaction torques within $0.23\mathrm{Nm}$.

</details>


### [128] [FoldQuantVLA: Native Low-Bit Quantization of Vision-Language-Action Models via Consistent Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24433)
*Hung T. Ho,Khanh D. Nguyen,Quang D. Nguyen,Thanh Q. Duong,Ngan Le,Meng Guo,Vien A. Ngo,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoldQuantVLA 是一种无需策略重训练的训练后量化框架，通过一致的激活表示和自定义 TensorRT 插件，实现四比特权重与激活（W4A4）的视觉-语言-动作推理加速，同时结合选择性 W8A8 保留以提升动作保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特视觉-语言-动作推理需要在降低观测到动作的延迟的同时保持机器人行为。四比特量化（W4A4）虽然在速度上有优势，但统一 W4A4 会降低动作保真度，因此需要一种在效率与保真度之间的平衡方案，且不希望进行策略重训练。

Method: FoldQuantVLA 是一个训练后量化框架，在标定、权值舍入和原生整数执行中保持一致的激活表示。它结合了通道缩放与块 Hadamard 变换，以及动态逐 token 量化，无需策略重训练。通过自定义 TensorRT 插件，在语言骨干和迭代动作专家的投影中以 W4A4 在 Ada GPU 和 Jetson AGX Orin 上执行；并选择性保留语言注意力输出和 FFN 下投影为八比特（W8A8）。

Result: 在 Orin 上相较浮点 TensorRT 获得 1.20–1.33× 加速，桌面端为 1.25–1.52×。选择性 W8A8 在全部四个检查点上提升了留出动作保真度。在四项真实机器人任务中，该配置将 GR00T N1.7 的成功率从统一 W4A4 的 80.0% 提升至 92.5%（每配置 80 次试验），Orin 延迟仅增加 1 ms。

Conclusion: FoldQuantVLA 表明，通过一致的激活表示、混合精度策略（W4A4 加选择性 W8A8）和原生整数执行，可以在不重训练策略的情况下实现低延迟且高保真度的视觉-语言-动作推理，适用于多种模型与真实机器人平台。

Abstract: Low-bit vision-language-action inference must reduce observation-to-action latency while preserving robot behavior. We present FoldQuantVLA, a post-training quantization framework that carries a consistent activation representation through calibration, weight rounding, and native integer execution. It combines channel scaling and block Hadamard transforms with dynamic per-token quantization, without policy retraining. Custom TensorRT plugins execute projections in both the language backbone and iterative action expert with four-bit weights and activations (W4A4) on Ada GPUs and Jetson AGX Orin. Evaluation spans LIBERO, SimplerEnv, and two robot platforms. Across three GR00T checkpoints and $π_{0.5}$, W4A4 achieves $1.20$ to $1.33\times$ speedups over floating-point TensorRT on Orin and $1.25$ to $1.52\times$ on desktop. Retaining language attention-output and feed-forward down projections at eight bits (W8A8) improves held-out action fidelity on all four checkpoints. Across four real-robot tasks, this configuration raises observed GR00T N1.7 success from $80.0\%$ with uniform W4A4 to $92.5\%$ over 80 trials per configuration, with a measured additional Orin latency of 1 ms.

</details>


### [129] [ScaleMPA: Rethinking Scalable RRT* Acceleration With a Grid-Native Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24497)
*Zilong Wang,Yuzhou Chen,Xinyue He,Chen Zhang,Guanghui He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ScaleMPA，用网格原生表示重构RRT*，通过多分辨率网格搜索引擎与哈希网格存储降低关键路径并暴露细粒度并行，在28nm工艺下实现毫秒级规划延迟，较现有加速器提速4.7–44.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模高维环境下的实时运动规划仍具挑战。已有RRT*加速方案采用树中心的状态组织，虽能降低单次查询成本，但端到端复杂度仍为超线性，且结构依赖限制了并行性。

Method: ScaleMPA采用网格原生表示替代层级遍历，通过直接网格访问缩短规划器关键路径并暴露细粒度并行；针对稀疏高维规划的实用性，进一步提出多分辨率网格搜索引擎与哈希网格存储系统，并以28nm CMOS实现。

Result: 在28nm CMOS工艺下，ScaleMPA实现毫秒级规划延迟，相较当前最先进的运动规划加速器取得4.7–44.4倍加速。

Conclusion: 以网格原生表示重构RRT*可同时降低关键路径并提升并行度，配合多分辨率搜索与哈希网格存储，可在高维稀疏场景中实现高效的实时运动规划加速。

Abstract: Real-time motion planning remains challenging in large and high-dimensional environments. Prior acceleration of RRT* follows tree-centric state organization, which reduces per-query cost but preserves superlinear end-to-end complexity and limits parallelism through structural dependencies. This paper presents ScaleMPA, a motion-planning accelerator that rethinks RRT* with a grid-native representation. By replacing hierarchical traversal with direct grid-based access, ScaleMPA reduces the planner critical path and exposes fine-grained parallelism. To make this reformulation practical under sparse high-dimensional planning, ScaleMPA further proposes a multi-resolution grid search engine and a hash-grid memory system. Implemented in 28 nm CMOS, ScaleMPA achieves millisecond-level planning latency and delivers 4.7$\times$--44.4$\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art motion-planning accelerators.

</details>


### [130] [A Monolithic Force-Proprioception Soft Acutuator Enabled by Single-Material 3D printing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24499)
*Nan Huang,Lele Liu,Junfeng Lu,Yipan Zhu,Jiansheng Dai,Sicong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种单材料、一步FDM成型的MFPS力-本体感知软体驱动器，集成非对称折纸弯曲气腔与力感知传感器，并验证了其抓取与自感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有气动本体感知驱动器多采用异质材料，易产生装配误差与应力集中，因此需要一种一体化、单材料的集成设计与制造方法。

Method: 基于导电TPU的应变电阻响应，设计并分析FPS传感器结构与感知特性，进行参数化分析；分析MFPS驱动器的FDM工艺参数，完成制造并对驱动与本体感知性能进行表征。

Result: MFPS驱动器实现40°弯曲角、12.5 N输出力；外力从0增至45 N时电阻变化26.9%；基于该驱动器开发两指力感知夹爪，并通过实验验证抓取与力感知能力。

Conclusion: MFPS单材料一体化设计与制造方法为自感知驱动器的发展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Pneumatic proprioceptive actuators integrate actuation and sensing for soft robots that attract interest due to functional potential. Existing approaches often suffer from assembly errors or stress concentrations caused by heterogeneous materials. In this work, we propose the Monolithic Force-Proprioception Soft (MFPS) design and fabrication method that integrates an Asymmetric Origami Bending (AOB) chamber and a Force-Proprioception Soft (FPS) sensor with single material through one-step Fused Deposition Modeling (FDM) fabrication. Based on the resistance response to strain of conductive thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU), we design and analyze the structure of the FPS sensor, and conduct parametric analysis on the sensing characteristics. The FDM fabrication parameters of the MFPS actuator are analyzed, followed by actuator fabrication and characterization of the actuation and proprioception performance. Experimental results show that the MFPS actuator achieves a bending angle of 40°, an output force of 12.5 N, and a resistance change of 26.9% as the applied external force increased from 0 to 45 N. A two-finger force-proprioception gripper is developed based on the MFPS actuator. The grasping and force-proprioception capabilities are experimentally validated, proving that the MFPS design method provides a new approach for the development of self-sensing actuators.

</details>


### [131] [TACIT: Tactile Contact Supervision for Spatial Attention in Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24507)
*Yanhou Lai,Fucai Zhu,Ruiqiang Wang,Koichi Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TACIT利用遥操作演示中测得的触觉接触来监督空间注意力，无需额外点标注，从而提升少样本视触觉策略对物体位置变化的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭少量演示训练的视觉运动策略往往只能复现演示轨迹，难以可靠适应物体位置变化；现有显式注意力方法依赖人工标注或视觉模型获取空间先验，成本高且受限。

Method: 用遥操作演示中测得的触觉接触，对先前相机点云生成高斯目标，监督一个注意力头；其池化输出条件化视触觉扩散策略。目标仅用于训练，推理时触觉观测仍作为输入。

Result: 真实机器人基准中，每任务仅10次演示、5个放置区域：TACIT在球体放置达66.7%成功、插peg达73.3%；输入匹配的3D视触觉融合分别为10.0%和20.0%，仅视觉DP3为20.0%和43.3%。每任务30次试验中TACIT均在12秒内进入150mm掌-物接近区，剩余失败均发生在到达之后。三个训练种子上优于输入匹配融合和架构匹配但无显式注意力监督的对照。接触前与接触时监督无一致顺序差异。

Conclusion: 在评估工作空间内，测得的触觉接触可为少样本演示提供有效的空间监督，改善接近行为，且监督贡献超出分支容量本身。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies trained from a few demonstrations may reproduce demonstrated trajectories without reliably following changes in object position. Existing approaches with explicit attention typically obtain spatial priors from human annotation or visual models. We introduce TACIT (tactile contact informs attention), which uses measured tactile contacts from teleoperated demonstrations to supervise spatial attention without additional point annotation. Gaussian targets over preceding camera point clouds supervise an attention head whose pooled output conditions a visuotactile diffusion policy. Targets are used only during training; tactile observations remain inputs at inference. In the primary real-robot benchmark, with ten demonstrations per task and five demonstrated placement regions, TACIT achieves 66.7% success on ball placement and 73.3% on peg insertion, compared with 10.0% and 20.0% for input-matched 3D visuotactile fusion and 20.0% and 43.3% for vision-only DP3. TACIT enters the 150 mm palm-to-object approach region within 12 seconds in all 30 trials per task; all remaining failures occur after arrival. Across three training seeds on real ball and simulated peg, TACIT outperforms input-matched fusion and an architecture-matched control without explicit attention supervision, supporting the contribution of supervision beyond branch capacity. Pre-contact and contact-time supervision show no consistent ordering. These results demonstrate that measured tactile contact provides effective spatial supervision for approach behavior from few demonstrations within the evaluated workspace.

</details>


### [132] [InsertAnything: Generalizable Contact-Rich Precision Insertion from Simulation to Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24511)
*Zhenghua Ma,Xinpan Meng,Zeyu Liu,Muyuan Ma,Hengdi Zhang,Houcheng Li,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种完全在仿真中训练、可零微调直接部署于真机的强化学习框架，用于接触丰富的精密轴孔插入，实现跨几何与跨间隙的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密插入对对齐误差敏感，易碰撞卡死；零件几何与间隙差异导致策略难以复用，且真机演示与微调成本高。

Method: 纯仿真强化学习：结合目标位姿与紧凑三维指尖力反馈，使策略在孔位估计有误差时搜索对齐并纠偏；采用解耦门控奖励协调对齐与插入；通过力信号平滑与状态无关标准差稳定训练。

Result: 在最小名义间隙0.02 mm的多种孔几何上完成真机插入，孔位误差下提升成功率并降低峰值接触力；跨间隙与跨几何评估验证泛化；在ManipulationNet轴孔基准Human-in-the-Loop协议下取得首个20/20满分（全自主插入）；仅用仿真六边形插入训练的单策略在8个未见真机任务上总体成功率95.0%。

Conclusion: 完全仿真学习可获得可直接部署、可跨真机任务复用的精密插入技能；项目已开源仿真与真机实验脚本、资产及训练权重。

Abstract: Contact-rich precision insertion is a key manipulation skill in robotic assembly. Tight clearances make insertion more sensitive to alignment errors and prone to collisions and jamming, while variations in geometry and clearance across parts further complicate policy reuse. We present a reinforcement learning framework that trains insertion policies entirely in simulation for direct deployment without real-world demonstrations or policy fine-tuning. By combining target poses with compact three-dimensional fingertip force feedback, the policy learns to search for alignment and correct its motion despite errors in the estimated hole position. A decoupled gated reward coordinates alignment and insertion. Force-signal smoothing and state-independent standard deviations stabilize the learning process. The resulting policies perform real-world insertion across multiple hole geometries with a minimum nominal clearance of 0.02 mm and improve success while reducing peak contact forces under hole-position errors. Cross-clearance and cross-geometry evaluations further confirm policy generalization. The system achieved the first perfect score of 20/20 on ManipulationNet's peg-in-hole benchmark under its Human-in-the-Loop protocol, with fully autonomous insertion motions. A single policy trained only on a simulated hexagonal insertion task achieved an overall success rate of 95.0% across eight unseen real-world insertion tasks. These results show that learning entirely in simulation can yield precision insertion skills that can be deployed directly and reused across real-world tasks. The project website (https://mzhsoul.github.io/InsertAnything/) provides open-source simulation and real-robot experiment scripts, assets, and trained checkpoints.

</details>


### [133] [Bridge3D: Enabling Vision-Language-Action Models to See and Act in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24525)
*Haoxuan Li,Sixu Yan,Lianghui Zhu,Xuanlai Tang,Shikang Wang,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bridge3D 将隐式与显式 3D 几何引导注入预训练 2D VLA 模型，实现"看"与"动"的 3D 化，显著提升空间敏感操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型主要基于 2D 观测训练，固有地限制其精细空间操作能力；此前方法虽引入隐式空间先验，但仍缺乏显式几何引导。

Method: 提出 Bridge3D，包含两种策略：1）Implicit Fusion，用 3D 基础模型特征丰富视觉 token，提升 3D"看"的能力；2）Explicit Conditioning，将动作去噪与显式 3D 语义场结合，实现 3D"动"的能力。此外采用逐层线性探测提升学习效率。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 基准上超过 π₀ 14.0 个百分点；真实世界实验中优于 Spatial Forcing 11.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: Bridge3D 在高精度、空间敏感的操作任务中展现出强大能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable generalization in robotic manipulation via large-scale multimodal pretraining. However, VLA models are mainly trained on 2D-centric observations, which inherently constrains their capacity for precise spatial manipulation. Previous methods enhance 3D awareness by introducing implicit spatial priors, but still lack explicit geometry guidance. In this paper, we propose Bridge3D that integrates both implicit and explicit 3D geometry guidance into pre-trained 2D VLA models, enabling them to ''see'' and ''act'' in 3D. Bridge3D introduces two strategies: 1) Implicit Fusion, which enriches visual tokens with features from 3D foundation models to improve ''seeing'' in 3D; 2) Explicit Conditioning, which integrates action denoising with an explicit 3D semantic field to achieve ''acting'' in 3D. Furthermore, we utilize the proposed layer-wise linear probing to improve learning efficiency. Experiments show that Bridge3D achieves superior performance against state-of-the-art methods. On the RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark, Bridge3D exceeds $π_0$ by 14.0 percentage points, while in real-world experiments, it outperforms Spatial Forcing by 11.7 percentage points. These results demonstrate Bridge3D's strong capabilities in high-precision and spatial-sensitive manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [134] [Estimation and Control of Tensegrity Manipulator Kinematics based on Strut Inclination Angles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24535)
*Tufail Ahmad Bhat,Shuhei Ikemoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于张拉整体连续机械臂形状与姿态控制的降阶模型，将机械臂建模为串联并联连杆机构，利用几何约束与IMU数据构建优化问题，首次在实物张拉整体机械臂上实现实时IMU形状估计并用PI控制器完成姿态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性连杆机器人由离散关节定义，而连续机器人因复杂连续弯曲构型难以表达，给闭环控制带来根本性挑战。现有连续机器人建模方法多假设存在连续弹性骨架，无法直接适用于由刚性杆件与张紧索网构成的张拉整体机械臂，因此张拉整体连续机器人的建模问题基本处于空白。

Method: 将张拉整体连续机械臂建模为串联连接的并联连杆机构，提出降阶模型。方法被构造为优化问题，结合张拉整体结构的几何约束与嵌入杆件中的IMU传感器信息进行求解，并采用简单PI控制器实现姿态控制。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够从任意静态构型出发，估计单模块张拉整体结构与多模块张拉整体机械臂的形状，并达到期望姿态。这是首次在全尺寸张拉整体机械臂上实现基于IMU的实时形状估计的实验验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为张拉整体连续机械臂的形状估计与姿态控制提供了一种可行的降阶建模与IMU融合方案，验证了实时形状估计与PI姿态控制的可行性，填补了张拉整体连续机器人建模与实验验证的空白。

Abstract: Unlike conventional rigid-link robots defined by discrete joints, continuum robots pose a fundamental challenge for expressing their complex continuous bending configurations for closed-loop control. Several modelling approaches have been proposed for conventional continuum robots, but tensegrity-based continuum robots remain largely open. Moreover, many of these approaches assume a continuous elastic backbone and are therefore not directly applicable to tensegrity manipulators, whose bodies are networks of rigid struts and tensioned cables. This work presents a reduced-order model for shape and posture control of a tensegrity-based continuum manipulator. The manipulator is modelled as a serially connected parallel-link mechanism. The proposed method is formulated as an optimization problem that uses geometric constraints of the tensegrity structure together with information from the Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) sensors embedded in the strut elements. To the best of our knowledge, this work presents the first experimental demonstration of a real-time IMU-based shape estimation method on a full-scale tensegrity manipulator and demonstrates posture control using a simple Proportional-Integral (PI) controller. The results show that the proposed method can estimate the shape of both single-module tensegrity structures and multi-module tensegrity manipulators from arbitrary static configurations and achieve desired postures.

</details>


### [135] [MIRA: Real-Time Full-Duplex Human-Robot Interaction for Embodied Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24547)
*Lijian Lin,Ye Zhu,Fan Zhang,Yunfei Liu,Baofeng Li,Xianwen Zeng,Jianan Wang,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIRA 是一个面向全身人形机器人的全双工具身陪伴交互统一框架，通过流式语音理解、响应文本与具身线索联合预测、滑动窗口动作生成以及双时间尺度交互策略，实现实时响应、可打断动作与物理安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统通常将对话编排与手势合成解耦，依赖完整音频进行离线动作生成，难以在增量输入和不确定的对话轮次边界下动态同步响应内容、韵律时序与物理安全。因此，需要研究部署机器人如何在流式交互中实现内容、时序与安全的一体化协同。

Method: 给定流式用户语音、对话历史与语音情感，MIRA 同时预测响应文本和显式具身线索。离散社交行为（如倾听、问候）映射到经过验证的机器人轨迹，开放式说话则配以流式共语动作。生成动作由“预测多于提交”的滑动窗口控制，提供时间前瞻以保持动作连续性，同时将物理执行限制在短且可取消的前缀内。此外设计 CORTEX 双时间尺度交互策略，管理低延迟流式处理与深思熟虑的轮次决策，并由机器人侧执行层在控制频率下强制物理安全约束。

Result: 在 Astribot S1 人形机器人上部署 MIRA。定量评估表明，其音频-动作对齐效果与当前最先进的动作生成基线相比具有竞争力，同时真实机器人部署测量刻画了流式响应能力与打断处理表现。

Conclusion: MIRA 将对话、具身动作生成与安全执行统一于全双工框架中，验证了在真实人形机器人上实现实时、表达性强且可打断的陪伴交互的可行性。

Abstract: Real-time embodied companion interaction requires a robot to infer user intent from streaming speech, generate timely responses, and execute expressive, interruptible motions. Existing systems typically decouple dialogue orchestration from gesture synthesis, relying on offline motion generation from complete audio. This separation leaves open how a deployed robot can dynamically synchronize response content, prosodic timing, and physical safety under incremental inputs and uncertain turn boundaries. We present MIRA, a unified framework for full-duplex embodied companion interaction. Given streaming user speech, dialogue history, and vocal affect, MIRA predicts both the response text and an explicit embodiment cue. Discrete social behaviors (\eg listening, greeting) are mapped to validated robot trajectories, while open-ended speaking is paired with streaming, co-speech motion. This generative motion is governed by a predict-more-than-commit sliding window that provides temporal look-ahead for motion continuity while limiting physical commitment to a short, cancellable prefix. Crucially, we design CORTEX, a dual-timescale interaction policy that manages low-latency streaming and deliberative turn decisions, backed by a robot-side execution layer that enforces physical safety constraints at the control rate. We deploy MIRA on an Astribot S1 humanoid robot. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate competitive audio-motion alignment relative to state-of-the-art motion-generation baselines, while real-robot deployment measurements characterize streaming responsiveness and interruption handling.

</details>


### [136] [Smoothness as a Constraint for Stable Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24552)
*Utsav Panchal,Denis Kleyko,Unal Artan,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeCap 是一种约束型强化学习算法，将全身平滑性解耦为上、下半身两组显式物理约束，并用有界障碍罚项提升约束满足度，在真实人形全身控制任务中显著降低上半身动作率与加速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界人形机器人的全身控制策略需兼顾任务响应性与物理平滑性。然而平滑需求在身体上并不一致：下半身需保持足够反应性，上半身需严格约束以维持稳定。现有强化学习多通过奖励函数中的辅助项施加平滑性，会与任务目标竞争，且将身体视为均质，无法直接控制决定平滑性的物理量。

Method: 提出 DeCap（解耦约束感知策略），一种约束型强化学习算法：将全身平滑性解耦为上半身与下半身两个独立约束组，每组把平滑性表述为对物理运动极限的显式约束；并引入有界障碍罚项，在接近极限时主动激活、在约束极限处保持有界，以改善可行性边界附近的约束满足。

Result: 在真实世界人形全身控制任务中，相比基于奖励的平滑策略，DeCap 将上半身动作率降低 2.50 倍、加速度降低 2.18 倍，同时改善下半身平滑性并减少瞬态运动。固定的一组平滑约束可跨多种地形迁移，减少大量奖励调参需求。

Conclusion: 通过将全身平滑性解耦为显式约束并采用有界障碍罚项，DeCap 在保证任务响应的同时实现了更优的全身平滑控制，并具备跨地形泛化能力，降低奖励调参成本。

Abstract: Embodied AI systems, particularly humanoid robots deployed in real world scenarios require whole-body control policies that are both task-responsive and physically smooth. However, smoothness is not uniform across the body: lower body must remain sufficiently reactive, while the upper body must be tightly regulated to preserve stability. Existing reinforcement learning approaches typically impose smoothness through auxiliary terms in the reward function, which compete with task objectives, treating the body as uniform and provide no direct control over the physical quantities responsible for smooth behavior. We introduce DeCap (Decoupled Constraint-aware policy), a constrained reinforcement learning algorithm that decouples whole-body smoothness into separate upper- and lower-body constraint groups, each formulates smoothness as explicit constraints on physical motion limits. To improve constraint satisfaction near feasibility boundaries, DeCap incorporates a bounded barrier penalty that activates proactively as limits are approached and remains bounded at the constraint limit. On real-world humanoid whole-body control task, DeCap reduces upper-body action rate by 2.50x and acceleration by 2.18x relative to reward-based smoothness policies, while also improving lower-body smoothness and reducing transient motion. We demonstrate that a fixed set of smoothness constraints transfers across diverse terrains, alleviating the need of extensive reward tuning.

</details>


### [137] [ARSTAG: An Agentic Real2Sim2Real System for Task-Specific Robot Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24563)
*Bowei Li,Yuner Zhang,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARSTAG：一个智能体化的Real2Sim2Real系统，用单张RGB图像和自然语言指令直接生成机器人策略学习数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略适应新操作任务通常需要大量人工工程或遥操作数据采集；仿真虽可规模化提供数据，但场景构建、专家行为设计和数据生成配置仍需逐任务投入大量精力。

Method: 提出ARSTAG系统，由语言智能体层级结构构建任务限定的仿真场景、生成机器人可行的演示，并通过任务一致随机化扩展训练分布；同时由协调智能体管理跨阶段反馈与恢复。输入为单张RGB图像加自然语言指令。

Result: 在涵盖抓取、放置、堆叠的七项操作任务上，生成演示使三种视觉运动策略架构成功完成sim-to-real迁移至双臂机器人，其中pi0.5平均真实成功率74.6%。消融显示任务一致随机化显著提升鲁棒性，且策略性能随生成数据集规模增大而提升。

Conclusion: ARSTAG可将单张图像和自然语言指令自动转化为机器人策略学习数据，减少逐任务人工工程，并有效支撑仿真到真实的策略迁移。

Abstract: Adapting visuomotor policies to new manipulation tasks often requires substantial manual engineering or teleoperated data collection. Simulation can provide task-specific data at scale, but constructing the scene, designing expert behavior, and configuring data generation still require significant per-task effort. We present ARSTAG, an agentic Real2Sim2Real system that turns a single RGB image and a natural-language instruction directly into robot policy-learning data. A hierarchy of language agents constructs a task-scoped simulation scene, generates robot-feasible demonstrations, and expands the training distribution through task-consistent randomization, while a coordinator agent manages cross-stage feedback and recovery. Across seven manipulation tasks spanning grasping, placement, and stacking, the ARSTAG-generated demonstrations enable sim-to-real transfer of three visuomotor policy architectures to a dual-arm robot, with pi0.5 achieving an average real-world success rate of 74.6%. Ablations show that task-consistent randomization substantially improves robustness, and policy performance increases with generated dataset size. Project webpage: https://boweili666.github.io/ARSTAG/.

</details>


### [138] [What do VLM-Based Vision-Language Navigation Models Rely on: Interpreting and Steering Policy Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24576)
*Débora Oliveira Makowski,Samiran Gode,Abhijeet Nayak,Marco Hutter,Cordelia Schmid,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言导航（VLN）模型的可解释性与可控性，通过干预式指标揭示其决策机制，并实现概念级和抽象行为层面的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLN模型依赖预训练的大型视觉语言模型来预测导航动作，这种多模态融合虽然支持多模态推理，但掩盖了信息如何在模态间路由、以及何种机制驱动导航决策。因此目前尚不清楚VLN模型是否基于相关语义线索做出预测，以及能否追踪任务进度。

Method: 采用基于干预的度量方法，衡量视觉观察、指令和视觉记忆对导航决策的因果影响；进一步分析模型内部激活，考察其对导航进度与语义结构的编码；并通过提取抽象行为的激活向量进行零样本迁移。

Result: 结果显示这些导航策略对所有输入模态均敏感，并不依赖单一模态；智能体编码了导航进度，并保留了来自VLM骨干的语义结构，从而支持通过内部激活进行概念级调控。提取的抽象行为激活向量能零样本迁移到分布外的真实场景，在无需额外微调的情况下提升性能。

Conclusion: VLN模型具有多模态敏感性、进度编码能力与语义可调控性，其内部激活可被用于概念级调控及抽象行为的零样本迁移，从而改善分布外真实场景的性能。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) models rely mostly on pre-trained large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to predict navigation actions. While this fusion of language instructions and visual observations allows multimodal reasoning, it obscures how information is routed across modalities or what mechanisms drive navigation decisions. Thus, it remains unclear whether VLN models ground their predictions in relevant semantic cues or can track task progress. In this work, we study the interpretability and steerability of VLN models. We use intervention-based metrics that measure how visual observations, instructions, and visual memory causally influence navigation decisions. Our results show that these navigation policies are sensitive to all input modalities and do not depend on a single one. We further show that these agents encode navigation progress and retain semantic structure from their VLM backbones, enabling concept-level steering through internal activations. Finally, we extract activation vectors for abstract behaviors to transfer them zero-shot to out-of-distribution real-world scenarios, improving performance without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [139] [Learning tactile perception from high-bandwidth single-point sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24621)
*Joseph Rigal,Emmanuel Virot,Caroline Pascal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SpectRobot 框架，将单点触觉信号转为时频谱图，用视觉编码器处理，实现高带宽接触动力学学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型机器人操作多依赖空间分布式触觉传感器（阵列），成本高、密度受限，且难以捕捉高频接触动力学。作者希望用稀疏单点高带宽测量替代空间密度扩展。

Method: 将单点触觉信号转换为紧凑的时频谱图（time-frequency spectrogram），编码高带宽触觉历史为固定尺寸的图像化表示；复用标准视觉编码器与视觉学习流程；传感器安装于远离接触面但机械耦合的位置，降低磨损。

Result: (1) 机器人可利用单点振动信号解决视觉遮挡的操作任务；(2) 时间历史显著影响策略性能，感知带宽决定可用频谱信息，测量达 100 kHz；(3) 同一表示可跨加速度、力、应变等不同触觉技术复用；常规现成硬件即可实现研究级仪器的能力。

Conclusion: 高带宽触觉感知的更广泛可及性有助于将接触动力学融入具身学习系统，并在某些任务中作为增加触觉空间密度的替代或补充方案。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is increasingly being incorporated into learning-based robotic manipulation, yet many existing approaches rely on spatially distributed sensors. Here we introduce SpectRobot, a framework that transforms single-point tactile signals into compact time-frequency spectrograms. These spectrograms encode high-bandwidth tactile histories as fixed-size image-like representations. They can be processed by standard vision encoders and integrated into learning pipelines originally developed for vision, while preserving temporal and frequency information unavailable to conventional cameras.
  Rather than increasing spatial density through arrays of tactile elements, SpectRobot exploits the rich dynamics contained in sparse, high-bandwidth single-point measurements. In our implementation, the sensors are mounted away from the contact surface while remaining mechanically coupled to it, reducing direct exposure to wear and potentially improving robustness in harsh environments and for long-term deployment on dexterous robots.
  Our experiments demonstrate that: (1) a robot can exploit single-point vibration signals to solve a visually occluded manipulation task; (2) temporal history strongly influences policy performance, while sensing bandwidth controls the spectral information available, with measurements extending to 100~kHz; and (3) the same representation can be used across different tactile sensing technologies mediated by acceleration, force, or strain. We further show that capabilities previously associated with research-grade instrumentation can be accessed using readily available, off-the-shelf hardware. We believe that broader access to high-bandwidth tactile sensing could facilitate the integration of contact dynamics into embodied learning systems and, for some tasks, offer an alternative or complement to increasing the spatial density of tactile sensing.

</details>


### [140] [From Semantic Decisions to Feasible Trajectories: Self-Evolving LLM-Guided Optimal Control for Narrow-Space Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24631)
*Zhengbao Yao,Yuanfu Luo,Kehan Xue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SE-LLM-OCP统一框架，用LLM做高层离散机动决策、最优控制模块保证低层动力学与碰撞约束，实现在狭窄非凸环境下的安全自动泊车并跨平台迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 狭窄非凸环境下的自动泊车仍具挑战：最优控制方法虽能显式施加车辆动力学与碰撞约束，但非凸性削弱求解器鲁棒性、易致失败；LLM虽具备强语义推理能力，但直接生成稠密轨迹难以保证物理可行性。因此需要结合二者优势。

Method: 提出SE-LLM-OCP统一框架：LLM负责高层离散机动决策，最优控制模块负责低层车辆动力学与碰撞约束。在线阶段，LLM 提出稀疏机动计划，将泊车任务分解为一系列短时域轨迹优化问题，由低层求解器顺序求解；若求解失败，LLM 汇总求解器与验证阶段的失败证据以指导重新规划。离线阶段，框架以累积的在线失败为驱动，从零自动演化出结构化决策知识库。

Result: 在类车车辆模型与差速驱动机器人的仿真中验证。结果表明，SE-LLM-OCP 能在狭窄场景中实现更安全的自动泊车，并证明同一机动表示可迁移到不同运动学平台。

Conclusion: 通过LLM高层推理与最优控制低层约束的分层耦合，以及失败驱动的在线重规划与离线知识库演化，SE-LLM-OCP有效提升了非凸狭窄环境泊车的安全性与跨平台可迁移性。

Abstract: Autonomous parking in nonconvex and narrow environments remains challenging. Although optimal-control methods can explicitly enforce vehicle dynamics and collision constraints, nonconvexity compromises solver robustness and can cause failures. Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong semantic reasoning capabilities, but directly generating dense trajectories makes it difficult to guarantee physical feasibility. We introduce SE-LLM-OCP, a unified framework in which LLMs make high-level discrete maneuver decisions, while an optimal-control module enforces low-level vehicle dynamics and collision constraints. Online, the LLM proposes sparse maneuver plans, decomposing the parking task into a sequence of short-horizon trajectory-optimization problems. A low-level solver then sequentially solves optimal-control problems. If the solver fails, the LLM aggregates failure evidence from the solver and validation stages to guide replanning. Offline, SE-LLM-OCP automatically evolves a structured decision-making knowledge base from scratch, driven by accumulated online failures. We validate our proposed framework in simulation on a car-like vehicle model and on a differential-drive robot. Our experimental results show that SE-LLM-OCP enables safer autonomous parking in narrow scenarios and demonstrates transfer of the same maneuver representation to a different kinematic platform.

</details>


### [141] [Feasibility Distance Fields for Heterogeneous Constraints in Robot Configuration Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24632)
*Xijing Cui,Huayan Pu,Jun Luo,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"可行性距离场"(FDF)，用统一的正定关节空间度量衡量到不可行配置集的剩余运动距离，使异构约束可统一比较与复合，并通过投影标签训练神经网络近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人机械臂的监测依赖各类约束专属指标，其单位与梯度尺度不可比，无法给出"违反前剩余多少构型空间运动"的统一度量。作者希望建立一个通用的构型空间余量，使外部/自碰撞、关节限位、灵巧度、柔顺性、力矩与动态可操作度等异构约束可在同一度量下比较与复合。

Method: 定义FDF为在固定正定关节空间度量下到不可行配置集并集的距离；借助经典距离集理论获得1-Lipschitz连续性、几乎处处可微与单位对偶梯度范数。进行可容许性分析，证明实用约束可定义非空闭集，并给出各约束的可容许公式（外/自碰撞、关节限位、灵巧度、笛卡尔与任务投影柔顺、载荷下关节力矩、动态可操作度）。同度量下异构约束通过逐点最小值复合，条件约束保持固定距离空间，多机器人约束产生块稀疏梯度以识别需反应的机器人。用基于投影的标签并配合距离损失与Eikonal惩罚训练神经网络近似。

Result: 在UR5e与双臂单元上评估七个场，使用值、投影、符号、梯度、复合与移动障碍物诊断。8,000个构型中最大可行侧割线比为0.920，学习梯度范数均值0.994–0.998，投影残差0.011–0.034 rad。24条随机障碍路径中，外部与复合碰撞场在3 cm内成功率90.4%与91.6%，符号错误率低于2%。

Conclusion: 结果支持存在统一的构型空间余量指标，并识别出近似误差主要出现在中轴附近与采样稀疏的边界区域。

Abstract: Robot manipulators are monitored by constraint-specific indicators whose units and gradient scales are not comparable, so they do not provide a common measure of the configuration-space motion remaining before violation. We define the feasibility distance field (FDF) as the distance, under a fixed positive-definite joint-space metric, to the union of infeasible configuration sets. Classical distance-to-set theory gives 1-Lipschitz continuity, almost-everywhere differentiability, and unit dual-gradient norm wherever the nearest projection is unique. The robotics contribution is an admissibility analysis showing when practical constraints define non-empty closed sets. We derive admissible formulations for external and self-collision, joint limits, dexterity, Cartesian and task-projected compliance, joint torque under payload, and dynamic manipulability. Since every field uses the same metric, heterogeneous constraints compose by a pointwise minimum, conditioned constraints retain a fixed distance space, and multi-robot constraints produce block-sparse gradients that identify which robots must react. We generate projection-based labels and train neural approximations with a distance loss and an Eikonal penalty. Simulations on a UR5e and a dual-arm cell evaluate seven fields using value, projection, sign, gradient, composition, and moving-obstacle diagnostics. Across 8,000 configurations, the largest feasible-side secant ratio is 0.920, mean learned gradient norms range from 0.994 to 0.998, and projection residuals range from 0.011 to 0.034 rad. Across 24 random obstacle paths, the external and composed collision fields achieve 90.4% and 91.6% success within 3 cm, with sign-error rates below 2%. The results support a common configuration-space margin and identify approximation errors near medial axes and sparsely sampled boundaries.

</details>


### [142] [Touch2Robot: Robot Touch in the Human Demonstration Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24660)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiaoyang Cheng,Hong Ying,Xiaoying Zhou,Jiaming Jiang,Haoran Guo,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Touch2Robot 让人类在采集演示时实时看到目标机器人手的接触情况，从而兼顾数据可扩展性与机器人可执行性，显著提升灵巧操作演示的质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示采集灵巧操作数据成本低、可扩展，但迁移到机器人手时常出现接触不稳定或不可行的问题；直接在目标机器人上采集虽避免了这种不匹配，却大幅提高了采集成本。论文旨在解决这一取舍问题。

Method: 论文提出 Touch2Robot 框架：在人类操作时采集人手运动、触觉手套测量和物体运动；用这些记录引导物体专属的强化学习策略复现演示的物体运动，并偏向与人类触觉一致的接触；再将学到的行为蒸馏为一个统一的实时重定向器，将人类观测和物体几何映射为机器人手构型；采集过程中在仿真中将预测的机器人构型与物体位姿同步以重建机器人-物体接触并可视化，帮助演示者调整后续交互。

Result: 在四个真实任务中，Touch2Robot 将平均真实机器人回放完成率从仅视觉反馈的 37.9% 提升到 72.1%；每次回放成功演示的采集时间从 58.6 秒降至 18.2 秒。重建的目标手接触相对真实机器人触觉测量达到 44.2% F1。用 Touch2Robot 演示训练的策略使下游 Diffusion Policy 性能比仅视觉反馈提升 29.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将机器人触觉引入人类演示循环，能同时提升可扩展灵巧数据采集的质量与效率。

Abstract: Human demonstrations offer a scalable way to collect manipulation data, but their contacts may be unstable or infeasible when transferred to a robot hand. Collecting demonstrations directly on the target robot avoids this mismatch, but substantially increases the cost of data collection. To address this trade-off, we present \textbf{Touch2Robot}, a framework that lets humans collect demonstrations while seeing how the target robot hand would contact the object. We capture human hand motion, tactile-glove measurements, and object motion during human manipulation. These recordings guide object-specific RL policies to reproduce the demonstrated object motion while favoring contacts consistent with the recorded human touch. We distill the learned behaviors into a unified real-time retargeter that maps incoming human observations and object geometry to robot hand configurations. During collection, the predicted robot configuration is synchronized with the tracked object pose in simulation to reconstruct robot-object contacts, which are visualized to help the demonstrator adapt subsequent interactions to the target hand. Across four real-world tasks, Touch2Robot improves average real-robot replay completion from 37.9\% to 72.1\% over visual-only feedback, while reducing the collection time per replay-successful demonstration from 58.6~s to 18.2~s. Reconstructed target-hand contacts achieve 44.2\% F1 against real-robot tactile measurements, and policies trained on Touch2Robot demonstrations improve downstream Diffusion Policy performance by 29.1 percentage points over visual-only feedback. These results show that bringing robot touch into the human demonstration loop improves both the quality and efficiency of scalable dexterous data collection. \textit{Project webpage: \href{https://Touch2Robot.github.io/}{https://Touch2Robot.github.io/}.}

</details>


### [143] [Think Like a World Model, Act Like a VLA: Distilling World-Model Representations into Compact Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24682)
*Trung Dao,Sankalp Yamsani,Jaden Park,Joohyung Kim,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过在世界模型上做一次特征对齐蒸馏，可让普通 VLA 策略继承其物理 grounding 而不承担生成未来的推理开销，实现低延迟且鲁棒的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型把观测映射到动作，缺乏对世界如何响应的目标，因此鲁棒性受限于数据覆盖；世界模型具备该目标但生成未来代价高（每步决策需数秒），无法进入控制回路。作者希望兼得二者。

Method: 在常规 VLA 训练中加入一个特征对齐项：先用冻结的世界模型对训练帧推理一次并缓存其特征，学生网络学习与该缓存一致。训练时不加载教师，投影器用后即弃，部署策略与未蒸馏基线完全相同。

Result: 部署仅需 32 ms、1.86 GB（消费级 RTX 5090）。0.8B 学生在 LIBERO 达 97.9%；RoboCasa-GR1 人形操作从 48.2% 提升至 50.5%；同样迁移到单臂与双臂真实硬件。增益在更换学生规模、骨干、对齐层与教师后仍成立。
